// Invoice PDF (FA-FIN-INVOICE).
//
// `render_invoice_pdf` is now a **preview only** (watermark "ENTWURF", no
// number, not stored). Issuing goes through `issue_invoice` in
// `commands::documents`, which allocates the RE number, validates GOZ /
// UStG rules, stores the document and writes the audit entry.

use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, Engine as _};
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tauri::State;

use crate::application::rbac;
use crate::commands::auth_commands::SessionState;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::pdf::Invoice;
use medoc_core::application::documents::issue::{self as svc, IssueInvoiceArgs};

#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, invoice))]
pub async fn render_invoice_pdf(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    patient_id: String,
    invoice: Invoice,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    let out = svc::issue_invoice(
        &pool,
        &session,
        IssueInvoiceArgs {
            patient_id,
            invoice,
            preview: true,
        },
    )
    .await?;
    STANDARD
        .decode(out.pdf_base64)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))
}

/// IPC commands for [`crate::commands::register`].
#[macro_export]
macro_rules! register_invoice_commands {
    () => {
        $crate::commands::invoice_commands::render_invoice_pdf,
    };
}

//! Issued invoice PDF snapshots (GoBD-oriented storage; replaces browser history).

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tauri::State;

use crate::application::rbac;
use crate::commands::auth_commands::SessionState;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::database::{audit_repo, invoice_document_repo};

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct InvoiceDocumentListRowDto {
    pub id: String,
    pub patient_id: String,
    pub document_number: String,
    pub payload_json: String,
    pub total_cents: i64,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub created_by: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct AppendInvoiceDocumentInput {
    pub id: String,
    pub patient_id: String,
    pub document_number: String,
    pub payload_json: String,
    pub total_cents: i64,
}

#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state))]
pub async fn list_invoice_documents(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    limit: Option<i64>,
) -> Result<Vec<InvoiceDocumentListRowDto>, AppError> {
    let _session = rbac::require(&session_state, "finance.read")?;
    let lim = limit.unwrap_or(200);
    let rows = invoice_document_repo::list_recent(&pool, lim).await?;
    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(|r| InvoiceDocumentListRowDto {
            id: r.id,
            patient_id: r.patient_id,
            document_number: r.document_number,
            payload_json: r.payload_json,
            total_cents: r.total_cents,
            created_at: r.created_at,
            created_by: r.created_by,
        })
        .collect())
}

#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, input))]
pub async fn append_invoice_document(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    input: AppendInvoiceDocumentInput,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require(&session_state, "finance.write")?;
    if input.id.trim().is_empty() || input.patient_id.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "id and patient_id are required".into(),
        ));
    }
    let doc_id = input.id.clone();
    let created_at = chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let excerpt = format!(
        "nr={} patient_id={} cents={}",
        input.document_number, input.patient_id, input.total_cents
    );
    let row = invoice_document_repo::InvoiceDocumentRow {
        id: input.id,
        patient_id: input.patient_id,
        document_number: input.document_number,
        payload_json: input.payload_json,
        total_cents: input.total_cents,
        created_at,
        created_by: session.user_id.clone(),
    };
    invoice_document_repo::insert_with_audit_trail(&pool, &row, &excerpt).await?;
    audit_repo::create(
        &pool,
        &session.user_id,
        "CREATE",
        "InvoiceDocument",
        Some(&doc_id),
        Some(&excerpt),
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(())
}

/// IPC commands for [`crate::commands::register`].
#[macro_export]
macro_rules! register_invoice_document_commands {
    () => {
        $crate::commands::invoice_document_commands::list_invoice_documents,
        $crate::commands::invoice_document_commands::append_invoice_document,
    };
}

//! Official documents: every certificate, prescription, receipt, invoice and
//! daily report goes through these commands. They allocate the number, store
//! the document (hash) in `issued_document` and write the audit entry.
//!
//! Permission checks per document kind happen in
//! [`medoc_core::application::documents::issue`]; the command only resolves
//! the session.

use serde::Deserialize;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tauri::State;

use crate::application::rbac;
use crate::commands::auth_commands::SessionState;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::database::issued_document_repo::{self, IssuedDocumentListRow};
use crate::infrastructure::pdf::ReportPdfInput;
use medoc_core::application::documents::issue::{
    self as svc, IssueClinicalArgs, IssueInvoiceArgs, IssuedPdf,
};

/// Certificate (incl. AU copies), prescription or receipt.
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, args))]
pub async fn issue_clinical_document(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    args: IssueClinicalArgs,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    svc::issue_clinical_document(&pool, &session, args).await
}

/// GOZ invoice. `preview = true` → watermarked draft, no number.
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, args))]
pub async fn issue_invoice(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    args: IssueInvoiceArgs,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    svc::issue_invoice(&pool, &session, args).await
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct CancelInvoiceArgs {
    pub document_id: String,
    pub reason: String,
}

/// Stornorechnung for an issued invoice.
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, args))]
pub async fn cancel_issued_invoice(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    args: CancelInvoiceArgs,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    svc::cancel_invoice(&pool, &session, &args.document_id, &args.reason).await
}

/// Print an issued document again ("KOPIE").
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state))]
pub async fn reprint_issued_document(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    document_id: String,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    svc::reprint_by_id(&pool, &session, &document_id).await
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct IssueReportArgs {
    pub input: ReportPdfInput,
    /// Daily report: the business date (one number per day).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub source_id: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub preview: bool,
}

/// Daily report and other practice reports as numbered PDFs.
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, args))]
pub async fn issue_report_pdf(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    args: IssueReportArgs,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    svc::issue_report(&pool, &session, args.input, args.source_id, args.preview).await
}

/// Issued documents of one patient (chart view) or recent ones (finance).
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state))]
pub async fn list_issued_documents(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    patient_id: Option<String>,
    kind: Option<String>,
    limit: Option<i64>,
) -> Result<Vec<IssuedDocumentListRow>, AppError> {
    match patient_id {
        Some(pid) => {
            rbac::require(&session_state, "patient.read")?;
            issued_document_repo::list_for_patient(&pool, &pid).await
        }
        None => {
            rbac::require(&session_state, "finance.read")?;
            issued_document_repo::list_recent(&pool, kind.as_deref(), limit.unwrap_or(200).min(1000))
                .await
        }
    }
}

/// Check whether a PDF (by SHA-256) was issued by this practice.
#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state))]
pub async fn verify_issued_document(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    sha256: String,
) -> Result<Option<IssuedDocumentListRow>, AppError> {
    rbac::require(&session_state, "audit.read")?;
    issued_document_repo::find_by_sha256(&pool, sha256.trim()).await
}

        $crate::commands::document_commands::cancel_issued_invoice,
        $crate::commands::document_commands::reprint_issued_document,
        $crate::commands::document_commands::issue_report_pdf,
        $crate::commands::document_commands::list_issued_documents,
        $crate::commands::document_commands::verify_issued_document,
    };
}

//! Template-editor preview for document templates.
//!
//! This path renders whatever the editor sends, so it is **never** an
//! official document: it always carries the "ENTWURF" watermark and has no
//! number. Real certificates / prescriptions / receipts are issued with
//! `issue_clinical_document` (server-side numbering, registry, RBAC).

use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, Engine as _};
use serde::Deserialize;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tauri::State;

use crate::application::rbac;
use crate::commands::auth_commands::SessionState;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::pdf::{render_clinical_layout_with_logo, ClinicalPdfLayout};
use crate::infrastructure::pdf_export::render_template_preview_pdf;
use medoc_core::application::documents::issue::WATERMARK_PREVIEW;
use medoc_core::application::documents::practice_profile::{
    document_locale, practice_logo, PracticeProfile,
};

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PreviewDocumentPdfArgs {
    pub kind: String,
    pub template_name: String,
    pub template_payload_json: String,
    pub body_lines: Vec<String>,
    /// Structured layout JSON (`ClinicalPdfLayout`); when set, overrides plain `body_lines` rendering.
    pub layout_json: Option<String>,
}

#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, args))]
pub async fn preview_document_pdf(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    args: PreviewDocumentPdfArgs,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    rbac::require(&session_state, "templates.read")?;
    let version: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&args.template_payload_json)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Template JSON: {e}")))?;
    let body_pt = version
        .get("bodyPt")
        .and_then(|x| x.as_u64())
        .map(|n| n.clamp(8, 18) as i32)
        .unwrap_or(11);
    let footer = version
        .get("footer")
        .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .chars()
        .take(240)
        .collect::<String>();

    let bytes = if let Some(json) = args.layout_json.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
        let mut layout: ClinicalPdfLayout = serde_json::from_str(json)
            .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("PDF-Layout: {e}")))?;
        // Practice identity from the database, as on the issued document.
        let profile = PracticeProfile::load(&pool).await?;
        let locale = document_locale(&pool).await;
        layout.practice_lines = profile.letterhead_lines();
        layout.footer_lines = profile.footer_lines(locale);
        layout.locale = Some(locale.into());
        layout.document_number = None;
        layout.watermark = Some(WATERMARK_PREVIEW.into());
        let logo = practice_logo(&pool).await;
        render_clinical_layout_with_logo(&layout, logo.as_ref())?
    } else {
        // Already watermarked inside `render_plain_preview`.
        render_template_preview_pdf(
            &args.kind,
            &args.template_name,
            &footer,
            body_pt,
            &args.body_lines,
            None,
        )?
    };
    Ok(STANDARD.encode(&bytes))
}

/// IPC commands for [`crate::commands::register`].
#[macro_export]
macro_rules! register_document_pdf_commands {
    () => {
        $crate::commands::document_pdf_commands::preview_document_pdf,
    };
}

//! Report PDF preview (statistics, balance, finance, audit, compliance).
//!
//! The permission depends on `input.reportKind` (audit reports need
//! `audit.read`, compliance reports `ops.system`, …). This command returns a
//! watermarked preview; numbered reports go through `issue_report_pdf`.

use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, Engine as _};
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tauri::State;

use crate::application::rbac;
use crate::commands::auth_commands::SessionState;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::pdf_export::ReportPdfInput;
use medoc_core::application::documents::issue as svc;

#[tauri::command]
#[tracing::instrument(level = "info", skip(pool, session_state, input))]
pub async fn render_report_pdf_command(
    pool: State<'_, SqlitePool>,
    session_state: State<'_, SessionState>,
    input: ReportPdfInput,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let session = rbac::require_authenticated(&session_state)?;
    let out = svc::issue_report(&pool, &session, input, None, true).await?;
    STANDARD
        .decode(out.pdf_base64)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))
}

/// IPC commands for [`crate::commands::register`].
#[macro_export]
macro_rules! register_report_pdf_commands {
    () => {
        $crate::commands::report_pdf_commands::render_report_pdf_command,
    };
}

================================================================================
FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/src/application/documents/issue.rs
================================================================================
//! Issuing official documents: one code path for numbering, rendering,
//! registry (hash), audit and reprints.
//!
//! Rules enforced here (not in the UI):
//! * RBAC per document kind (certificates/prescriptions: physicians only).
//! * Practice identity comes from the database, never from the request.
//! * Every issued document gets a gap-free yearly number from
//!   `document_sequence`, is stored verbatim in `issued_document` with the
//!   SHA-256 of the PDF, and is audited.
//! * A second request for the same source record is a **reprint**: rendered
//!   from the stored payload with a "KOPIE" watermark, never a new number.
//! * Previews carry an "ENTWURF" watermark, get no number and are not stored.
//! * Numbers are only allocated on the MASTER device.

use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, Engine as _};
use chrono::Datelike;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use super::practice_profile::{document_locale, practice_logo, PracticeProfile};
use crate::application::auth_service::Session;
use crate::application::rbac::{effective_allowed, Role};
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::database::issued_document_repo::{
    self as repo, sha256_hex, IssuedDocument,
};
use crate::infrastructure::database::{audit_repo, invoice_document_repo, sync_outbox};
use crate::infrastructure::pdf::{
    is_winansi_renderable, render_clinical_layout_with_logo, render_goz_invoice, render_report_pdf,
    validate_invoice, ClinicalPdfLayout, Invoice, InvoiceKind, ReportPdfInput,
};

pub const WATERMARK_PREVIEW: &str = "ENTWURF";
pub const WATERMARK_COPY: &str = "KOPIE";

/// Kinds stored in `issued_document.kind`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum DocKind {
    Invoice,
    Cancellation,
    Receipt,
    Certificate,
    Prescription,
    ChartExtract,
    DischargeLeaflet,
    DailyReport,
    Report,
}

impl DocKind {
    pub fn as_db(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            DocKind::Invoice => "INVOICE",
            DocKind::Cancellation => "CANCELLATION",
            DocKind::Receipt => "RECEIPT",
            DocKind::Certificate => "CERTIFICATE",
            DocKind::Prescription => "PRESCRIPTION",
            DocKind::ChartExtract => "CHART_EXTRACT",
            DocKind::DischargeLeaflet => "DISCHARGE_LEAFLET",
            DocKind::DailyReport => "DAILY_REPORT",
            DocKind::Report => "REPORT",
        }
    }

    pub fn from_db(s: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        Some(match s {
            "INVOICE" => DocKind::Invoice,
            "CANCELLATION" => DocKind::Cancellation,
            "RECEIPT" => DocKind::Receipt,
            "CERTIFICATE" => DocKind::Certificate,
            "PRESCRIPTION" => DocKind::Prescription,
            "CHART_EXTRACT" => DocKind::ChartExtract,
            "DISCHARGE_LEAFLET" => DocKind::DischargeLeaflet,
            "DAILY_REPORT" => DocKind::DailyReport,
            "REPORT" => DocKind::Report,
            _ => return None,
        })
    }

    /// Number prefix: `RE-2026-000001`.
    pub fn prefix(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            DocKind::Invoice => "RE",
            DocKind::Cancellation => "ST",
            DocKind::Receipt => "QU",
            DocKind::Certificate => "AT",
            DocKind::Prescription => "RZ",
            DocKind::ChartExtract => "AK",
            DocKind::DischargeLeaflet => "NB",
            DocKind::DailyReport => "TB",
            DocKind::Report => "BE",
        }
    }

    /// Permission required to issue / reprint.
    pub fn issue_permission(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            DocKind::Certificate | DocKind::Prescription | DocKind::DischargeLeaflet => {
                "patient.write_medical"
            }
            DocKind::ChartExtract => "patient.read",
            DocKind::Invoice | DocKind::Cancellation | DocKind::Receipt => "finance.write",
            DocKind::DailyReport => "finance.day_close.write",
            DocKind::Report => "finance.read",
        }
    }

    /// Permission required for an (unnumbered, watermarked) preview.
    pub fn preview_permission(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            DocKind::Certificate | DocKind::Prescription | DocKind::DischargeLeaflet => {
                "patient.read_medical"
            }
            DocKind::ChartExtract => "patient.read",
            _ => "finance.read",
        }
    }

    fn clinical(kind: &str) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        match kind {
            "certificate" => Ok(DocKind::Certificate),
            "prescription" => Ok(DocKind::Prescription),
            "receipt" => Ok(DocKind::Receipt),
            _ => Err(AppError::validation_code("error.documents.unknown_kind")),
        }
    }
}

pub fn require_permission(session: &Session, action: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let role = Role::parse(&session.role).ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
    if effective_allowed(action, role, &session.permission_overrides) {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(AppError::Forbidden)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct IssuedPdf {
    pub document_id: Option<String>,
    pub document_number: Option<String>,
    pub sha256: String,
    pub pdf_base64: String,
    /// True when an existing document was printed again ("KOPIE").
    pub reprint: bool,
}

fn now_iso() -> String {
    chrono::Utc::now()
        .format("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%.3fZ")
        .to_string()
}

fn year_now() -> i32 {
    chrono::Local::now().year()
}

/// Rejects any string in the payload that the PDF fonts cannot render.
fn check_renderable(v: &Value) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let ok = match v {
        Value::String(s) => is_winansi_renderable(s),
        Value::Array(a) => return a.iter().try_for_each(check_renderable),
        Value::Object(o) => return o.values().try_for_each(check_renderable),
        _ => true,
    };
    if ok {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(AppError::validation_code(
            "error.documents.unsupported_characters",
        ))
    }
}

/// Run `body` inside `BEGIN IMMEDIATE … COMMIT` on one pooled connection.
macro_rules! immediate_tx {
    ($pool:expr, |$conn:ident| $body:block) => {{
        let mut conn_guard = $pool.acquire().await?;
        sqlx::query("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            .execute(&mut *conn_guard)
            .await?;
        let result: Result<_, AppError> = async {
            let $conn: &mut sqlx::SqliteConnection = &mut *conn_guard;
            $body
        }
        .await;
        match result {
            Ok(v) => {
                sqlx::query("COMMIT").execute(&mut *conn_guard).await?;
                Ok::<_, AppError>(v)
            }
            Err(e) => {
                let _ = sqlx::query("ROLLBACK").execute(&mut *conn_guard).await;
                Err(e)
            }
        }
    }};
}

async fn after_insert(pool: &SqlitePool, session: &Session, doc: &IssuedDocument, action: &str) {
    // Replicate to peers (MASTER → REPLICA) and audit. Failures here must
    // not undo an issued document, so they are logged, not returned.
    let body = serde_json::to_string(doc).unwrap_or_default();
    if let Err(e) =
        sync_outbox::record_or_noop(pool, "issued_document", &doc.id, "INSERT", &body).await
    {
        tracing::warn!(event = "ISSUED_DOCUMENT_OUTBOX_FAILED", error = %e);
    }
    let details = format!(
        "nr={} sha256={} patient={}",
        doc.document_number,
        doc.sha256,
        doc.patient_id.as_deref().unwrap_or("-")
    );
    if let Err(e) = audit_repo::create(
        pool,
        &session.user_id,
        action,
        doc.kind.as_str(),
        doc.source_id.as_deref().or(Some(doc.id.as_str())),
        Some(&details),
    )
    .await
    {
        tracing::error!(event = "ISSUED_DOCUMENT_AUDIT_FAILED", error = %e);
    }
}

// ===========================================================================
// Clinical documents: certificate, prescription, receipt
// ===========================================================================

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct IssueClinicalArgs {
    /// `certificate` | `prescription` | `receipt`
    pub kind: String,
    /// `certificate.id` | `prescription.id` | `payment.id`
    pub source_id: String,
    /// `ClinicalPdfLayout` JSON built by the frontend (content only).
    pub layout_json: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub preview: bool,
    /// Certificates: `patient` (default) | `employer` | `insurer`.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub copy_variant: Option<String>,
}

/// Checks the source record and returns its patient id.
async fn load_clinical_source(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    kind: DocKind,
    source_id: &str,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    match kind {
        DocKind::Certificate => {
            let row: Option<(String,)> =
                sqlx::query_as("SELECT patient_id FROM certificate WHERE id = ?1")
                    .bind(source_id)
                    .fetch_optional(pool)
                    .await?;
            row.map(|r| r.0)
                .ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Certificate".into()))
        }
        DocKind::Prescription => {
            // One prescription or a combined sheet (comma-separated ids).
            let mut patient: Option<String> = None;
            for id in source_id.split(',') {
                let row: Option<(String, Option<String>)> = sqlx::query_as(
                    "SELECT patient_id, prescription_type FROM prescription WHERE id = ?1",
                )
                .bind(id)
                .fetch_optional(pool)
                .await?;
                let (patient_id, kind) = row.ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Prescription".into()))?;
                match kind
                    .as_deref()
                    .unwrap_or("PRIVAT")
                    .trim()
                    .to_uppercase()
                    .as_str()
                {
                    // BtM needs the official BtM form (BtMVV), never plain paper.
                    "BTM" => {
                        return Err(AppError::validation_code(
                            "error.documents.btm_requires_official_form",
                        ))
                    }
                    // Statutory patients: prescription-only drugs go via E-Rezept (TI).
                    "KASSE" => {
                        return Err(AppError::validation_code(
                            "error.documents.gkv_requires_erezept",
                        ))
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
                match &patient {
                    Some(p) if p != &patient_id => {
                        return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.documents.mixed_patients"))
                    }
                    _ => patient = Some(patient_id),
                }
            }
            patient.ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Prescription".into()))
        }
        DocKind::Receipt => {
            let row: Option<(String, String)> =
                sqlx::query_as("SELECT patient_id, status FROM payment WHERE id = ?1")
                    .bind(source_id)
                    .fetch_optional(pool)
                    .await?;
            let (patient_id, status) = row.ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Payment".into()))?;
            if status != "PAID" && status != "PARTIALLY_PAID" {
                return Err(AppError::validation_code(
                    "error.documents.receipt_requires_payment",
                ));
            }
            Ok(patient_id)
        }
        _ => Err(AppError::validation_code("error.documents.unknown_kind")),
    }
}

fn normalize_copy_variant(kind: DocKind, v: Option<&str>) -> Result<&'static str, AppError> {
    if kind != DocKind::Certificate {
        return Ok("ORIGINAL");
    }
    match v.unwrap_or("patient") {
        "patient" => Ok("patient"),
        "employer" => Ok("employer"),
        "insurer" => Ok("insurer"),
        _ => Err(AppError::validation_code(
            "error.documents.unknown_copy_variant",
        )),
    }
}

/// Apply server-side facts to the client layout (as JSON so the exact
/// payload can be stored and re-rendered later).
fn apply_server_fields(
    layout: &mut Value,
    profile: &PracticeProfile,
    locale: &str,
    copy_variant: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let obj = layout
        .as_object_mut()
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Validation("layout must be a JSON object".into()))?;
    obj.insert(
        "practiceLines".into(),
        serde_json::json!(profile.letterhead_lines()),
    );
    obj.insert(
        "footerLines".into(),
        serde_json::json!(profile.footer_lines(locale)),
    );
    let sig = profile.signature_lines();
    if !sig.is_empty() {
        obj.insert("signatureLines".into(), serde_json::json!(sig));
    }
    obj.insert("locale".into(), Value::String(locale.to_string()));
    obj.insert("rtl".into(), Value::Bool(false));
    obj.remove("logoKvJson");
    obj.remove("watermark");
    obj.remove("documentNumber");
    if copy_variant != "ORIGINAL" {
        obj.insert(
            "copyVariant".into(),
            Value::String(copy_variant.to_string()),
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Patient identity panel from the database (never from the client):
/// certificates and prescriptions must name the patient exactly as recorded.
async fn patient_panel(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    patient_id: &str,
    locale: &str,
    copy_variant: &str,
) -> Result<Value, AppError> {
    let row: Option<(String, Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>)> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT name, CAST(date_of_birth AS TEXT), insurance_number, address FROM patient WHERE id = ?1",
    )
    .bind(patient_id)
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await?;
    let (name, dob, ins, addr) = row.ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Patient".into()))?;
    let de = locale == "de";
    let dob = dob
        .as_deref()
        .and_then(|d| chrono::NaiveDate::parse_from_str(d.get(..10)?, "%Y-%m-%d").ok())
        .map(|d| d.format("%d.%m.%Y").to_string());
    let mut left = vec![name.trim().to_string()];
    if let Some(d) = dob {
        left.push(format!("{} {d}", if de { "Geb.-Datum:" } else { "Date of birth:" }));
    }
    // The employer copy shows who is unfit for work, nothing more.
    if copy_variant != "employer" {
        if let Some(i) = ins.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {
            left.push(format!("{} {i}", if de { "Vers.-Nr.:" } else { "Insurance no.:" }));
        }
    }
    let right: Vec<String> = addr
        .as_deref()
        .map(|a| {
            a.split(['\n', ','])
                .map(str::trim)
                .filter(|l| !l.is_empty())
                .map(String::from)
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default();
    Ok(serde_json::json!({
        "leftTitle": if de { "Patient/in" } else { "Patient" },
        "leftLines": left,
        "rightTitle": if de { "Anschrift" } else { "Address" },
        "rightLines": if right.is_empty() { vec!["-".to_string()] } else { right },
    }))
}

fn render_layout_value(
    v: &Value,
    logo: Option<&crate::infrastructure::pdf::PdfLogo>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let layout: ClinicalPdfLayout = serde_json::from_value(v.clone())
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("PDF-Layout: {e}")))?;
    render_clinical_layout_with_logo(&layout, logo)
}

pub async fn issue_clinical_document(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    args: IssueClinicalArgs,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let kind = DocKind::clinical(args.kind.trim())?;
    let mut args = args;
    if kind == DocKind::Prescription {
        // Canonical key for combined prescriptions: sorted, unique ids.
        let mut ids: Vec<&str> = args
            .source_id
            .split(',')
            .map(str::trim)
            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
            .collect();
        ids.sort_unstable();
        ids.dedup();
        args.source_id = ids.join(",");
    }
    if args.preview {
        require_permission(session, kind.preview_permission())?;
    } else {
        require_permission(session, kind.issue_permission())?;
    }
    let patient_id = load_clinical_source(pool, kind, &args.source_id).await?;
    let copy_variant = normalize_copy_variant(kind, args.copy_variant.as_deref())?;
    let profile = PracticeProfile::load(pool).await?;
    profile.require_complete()?;
    let locale = document_locale(pool).await;
    let logo = practice_logo(pool).await;

    let mut layout: Value = serde_json::from_str(&args.layout_json)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("PDF-Layout: {e}")))?;
    apply_server_fields(&mut layout, &profile, locale, copy_variant)?;
    if matches!(kind, DocKind::Certificate | DocKind::Prescription) {
        let panel = patient_panel(pool, &patient_id, locale, copy_variant).await?;
        if let Some(obj) = layout.as_object_mut() {
            obj.insert("twoColumn".into(), panel);
        }
    }
    check_renderable(&layout)?;

    if args.preview {
        layout["watermark"] = Value::String(WATERMARK_PREVIEW.into());
        let bytes = render_layout_value(&layout, logo.as_ref())?;
        return Ok(IssuedPdf {
            document_id: None,
            document_number: None,
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
            reprint: false,
        });
    }

    repo::ensure_numbering_authority(pool).await?;

    // Same source + copy already issued → reprint the stored version.
    if let Some(existing) =
        repo::find_by_source(pool, kind.as_db(), &args.source_id, copy_variant).await?
    {
        return reprint(pool, session, &existing).await;
    }

    let issued_at = now_iso();
    let doc = immediate_tx!(pool, |conn| {
        let number = repo::next_number(conn, kind.prefix(), year_now()).await?;
        layout["documentNumber"] = Value::String(number.clone());
        let bytes = render_layout_value(&layout, logo.as_ref())?;
        let doc = IssuedDocument {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),
            kind: kind.as_db().into(),
            document_number: number,
            source_table: Some(
                match kind {
                    DocKind::Certificate => "certificate",
                    DocKind::Prescription => "prescription",
                    _ => "payment",
                }
                .into(),
            ),
            source_id: Some(args.source_id.clone()),
            copy_variant: copy_variant.into(),
            patient_id: Some(patient_id.clone()),
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            payload_json: layout.to_string(),
            status: "ISSUED".into(),
            cancels_id: None,
            issued_by: session.user_id.clone(),
            issued_at: issued_at.clone(),
            updated_at: issued_at.clone(),
        };
        repo::insert(conn, &doc).await?;
        Ok((doc, bytes))
    })?;
    let (doc, bytes) = doc;
    after_insert(pool, session, &doc, "ISSUE_DOCUMENT").await;
    Ok(IssuedPdf {
        document_id: Some(doc.id),
        document_number: Some(doc.document_number),
        sha256: doc.sha256,
        pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
        reprint: false,
    })
}

// ===========================================================================
// Invoices (GOZ) and cancellation invoices
// ===========================================================================

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct IssueInvoiceArgs {
    pub patient_id: String,
    /// Content from the UI (lines, recipient, note). Practice identity,
    /// patient identity and number are overwritten on the server.
    pub invoice: Invoice,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub preview: bool,
}

async fn complete_invoice_from_db(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    inv: &mut Invoice,
    patient_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let profile = PracticeProfile::load(pool).await?;
    profile.require_complete()?;
    inv.practice_name = profile.name.trim().to_string();
    inv.practice_address = profile.address_lines();
    inv.practice_bsnr = profile.bsnr.clone().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty());
    inv.clinician_zanr = profile.zanr.clone().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty());
    if inv
        .clinician_name
        .as_deref()
        .map(str::trim)
        .unwrap_or("")
        .is_empty()
    {
        inv.clinician_name = profile.clinician_name.clone();
    }
    inv.tax_number = profile.tax_number.clone().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty());
    inv.vat_id = profile.vat_id.clone().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty());
    let bank = profile.bank_lines();
    inv.bank_details = if bank.is_empty() { None } else { Some(bank) };
    inv.practice_lines = profile.letterhead_lines();
    inv.footer_lines = profile.footer_lines("de");
    if inv
        .vat_notice
        .as_deref()
        .map(str::trim)
        .unwrap_or("")
        .is_empty()
    {
        inv.vat_notice = Some(profile.vat_notice());
    }
    if inv
        .payment_terms_text
        .as_deref()
        .map(str::trim)
        .unwrap_or("")
        .is_empty()
    {
        inv.payment_terms_text = Some(profile.payment_terms_text());
    }
    let (name, dob): (String, String) =
        sqlx::query_as("SELECT name, CAST(date_of_birth AS TEXT) FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(patient_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await?
            .ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Patient".into()))?;
    inv.patient_name = name;
    inv.patient_birth_date = Some(dob.chars().take(10).collect());
    inv.logo = practice_logo(pool).await;
    inv.locale = "de".into();
    inv.rtl = false;
    Ok(())
}

fn invoice_payload(inv: &Invoice) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    serde_json::to_string(inv).map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))
}

async fn mirror_into_invoice_documents(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    doc: &IssuedDocument,
    total_cents: i64,
) {
    // Keep the existing finance list (invoice_document) in sync.
    let row = invoice_document_repo::InvoiceDocumentRow {
        id: doc.id.clone(),
        patient_id: doc.patient_id.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
        document_number: doc.document_number.clone(),
        payload_json: doc.payload_json.clone(),
        total_cents,
        created_at: doc.issued_at.clone(),
        created_by: session.user_id.clone(),
    };
    let excerpt = format!("nr={} cents={total_cents}", doc.document_number);
    if let Err(e) = invoice_document_repo::insert_with_audit_trail(pool, &row, &excerpt).await {
        tracing::error!(event = "INVOICE_DOCUMENT_MIRROR_FAILED", error = %e);
    }
}

pub async fn issue_invoice(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    args: IssueInvoiceArgs,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let IssueInvoiceArgs {
        patient_id,
        mut invoice,
        preview,
    } = args;
    require_permission(
        session,
        if preview {
            DocKind::Invoice.preview_permission()
        } else {
            DocKind::Invoice.issue_permission()
        },
    )?;
    complete_invoice_from_db(pool, &mut invoice, &patient_id).await?;
    invoice.kind = InvoiceKind::Invoice;
    invoice.cancels_number = None;
    invoice.cancels_date = None;
    if invoice.date.trim().is_empty() {
        invoice.date = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string();
    }

    if preview {
        invoice.number = WATERMARK_PREVIEW.into();
        validate_invoice(&invoice)?;
        invoice.watermark = Some(WATERMARK_PREVIEW.into());
        let bytes = render_goz_invoice(&invoice)?;
        return Ok(IssuedPdf {
            document_id: None,
            document_number: None,
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
            reprint: false,
        });
    }

    repo::ensure_numbering_authority(pool).await?;
    let issued_at = now_iso();
    let (doc, bytes, total) = immediate_tx!(pool, |conn| {
        invoice.number = repo::next_number(conn, DocKind::Invoice.prefix(), year_now()).await?;
        // Validation failure rolls back → the number is not consumed (gap-free).
        validate_invoice(&invoice)?;
        let bytes = render_goz_invoice(&invoice)?;
        let doc = IssuedDocument {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),
            kind: DocKind::Invoice.as_db().into(),
            document_number: invoice.number.clone(),
            source_table: Some("patient".into()),
            source_id: None,
            copy_variant: "ORIGINAL".into(),
            patient_id: Some(patient_id.clone()),
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            payload_json: invoice_payload(&invoice)?,
            status: "ISSUED".into(),
            cancels_id: None,
            issued_by: session.user_id.clone(),
            issued_at: issued_at.clone(),
            updated_at: issued_at.clone(),
        };
        repo::insert(conn, &doc).await?;
        Ok((doc, bytes, invoice.total_cents()))
    })?;
    after_insert(pool, session, &doc, "ISSUE_DOCUMENT").await;
    mirror_into_invoice_documents(pool, session, &doc, total).await;
    Ok(IssuedPdf {
        document_id: Some(doc.id),
        document_number: Some(doc.document_number),
        sha256: doc.sha256,
        pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
        reprint: false,
    })
}

/// Stornorechnung: new document with negative lines that references the
/// original. The original is marked CANCELLED, never changed or deleted.
pub async fn cancel_invoice(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    document_id: &str,
    reason: &str,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    require_permission(session, DocKind::Cancellation.issue_permission())?;
    if reason.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code(
            "error.documents.cancel_reason_required",
        ));
    }
    let original = repo::find_by_id(pool, document_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Document".into()))?;
    if original.kind != DocKind::Invoice.as_db() || original.status != "ISSUED" {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.documents.not_cancellable"));
    }
    repo::ensure_numbering_authority(pool).await?;

    let mut inv: Invoice = serde_json::from_str(&original.payload_json)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("stored invoice: {e}")))?;
    inv.kind = InvoiceKind::Cancellation;
    inv.cancels_number = Some(original.document_number.clone());
    inv.cancels_date = Some(inv.date.clone());
    inv.date = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string();
    inv.note = Some(format!("Grund der Stornierung: {}", reason.trim()));
    for l in &mut inv.lines {
        l.amount_cents = -l.amount_cents.abs();
    }
    inv.logo = practice_logo(pool).await;

    let issued_at = now_iso();
    let (doc, bytes, total) = immediate_tx!(pool, |conn| {
        inv.number = repo::next_number(conn, DocKind::Cancellation.prefix(), year_now()).await?;
        validate_invoice(&inv)?;
        let bytes = render_goz_invoice(&inv)?;
        let doc = IssuedDocument {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),
            kind: DocKind::Cancellation.as_db().into(),
            document_number: inv.number.clone(),
            source_table: Some("issued_document".into()),
            source_id: Some(original.id.clone()),
            copy_variant: "ORIGINAL".into(),
            patient_id: original.patient_id.clone(),
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            payload_json: invoice_payload(&inv)?,
            status: "ISSUED".into(),
            cancels_id: Some(original.id.clone()),
            issued_by: session.user_id.clone(),
            issued_at: issued_at.clone(),
            updated_at: issued_at.clone(),
        };
        repo::insert(conn, &doc).await?;
        repo::mark_cancelled(conn, &original.id).await?;
        Ok((doc, bytes, inv.total_cents()))
    })?;
    after_insert(pool, session, &doc, "CANCEL_DOCUMENT").await;
    mirror_into_invoice_documents(pool, session, &doc, total).await;
    Ok(IssuedPdf {
        document_id: Some(doc.id),
        document_number: Some(doc.document_number),
        sha256: doc.sha256,
        pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
        reprint: false,
    })
}

// ===========================================================================
// Reports (daily report, statistics, balance …)
// ===========================================================================

/// Permission per report kind (`ReportPdfInput.report_kind`).
pub fn report_permission(report_kind: Option<&str>) -> &'static str {
    match report_kind.unwrap_or("finance") {
        "audit" => "audit.read",
        "compliance" => "ops.system",
        "statistics" => "statistics.read",
        "daily_report" => "finance.day_close.write",
        _ => "finance.read",
    }
}

/// Daily reports are official (one number per day, reprints are copies);
/// other reports are numbered exports.
pub async fn issue_report(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    mut input: ReportPdfInput,
    source_id: Option<String>,
    preview: bool,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    require_permission(session, report_permission(input.report_kind.as_deref()))?;
    let kind = if input.report_kind.as_deref() == Some("daily_report") {
        DocKind::DailyReport
    } else {
        DocKind::Report
    };
    let profile = PracticeProfile::load(pool).await?;
    profile.require_complete()?;
    input.practice_name = profile.name.trim().to_string();
    input.practice_address = profile.address_lines();
    input.locale = Some(document_locale(pool).await.into());

    if preview {
        input.watermark = Some(WATERMARK_PREVIEW.into());
        input.document_number = None;
        let bytes = render_report_pdf(&input)?;
        return Ok(IssuedPdf {
            document_id: None,
            document_number: None,
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
            reprint: false,
        });
    }
    repo::ensure_numbering_authority(pool).await?;
    if let Some(sid) = source_id.as_deref() {
        if let Some(existing) = repo::find_by_source(pool, kind.as_db(), sid, "ORIGINAL").await? {
            return reprint(pool, session, &existing).await;
        }
    }
    let issued_at = now_iso();
    let (doc, bytes) = immediate_tx!(pool, |conn| {
        let number = repo::next_number(conn, kind.prefix(), year_now()).await?;
        input.document_number = Some(number.clone());
        input.watermark = None;
        let bytes = render_report_pdf(&input)?;
        let doc = IssuedDocument {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),
            kind: kind.as_db().into(),
            document_number: number,
            source_table: source_id.as_ref().map(|_| "day_close".to_string()),
            source_id: source_id.clone(),
            copy_variant: "ORIGINAL".into(),
            patient_id: None,
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            payload_json: serde_json::to_string(&input)
                .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))?,
            status: "ISSUED".into(),
            cancels_id: None,
            issued_by: session.user_id.clone(),
            issued_at: issued_at.clone(),
            updated_at: issued_at.clone(),
        };
        repo::insert(conn, &doc).await?;
        Ok((doc, bytes))
    })?;
    after_insert(pool, session, &doc, "ISSUE_DOCUMENT").await;
    Ok(IssuedPdf {
        document_id: Some(doc.id),
        document_number: Some(doc.document_number),
        sha256: doc.sha256,
        pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
        reprint: false,
    })
}

/// Register a document rendered elsewhere (chart extract, discharge leaflet):
/// allocates the number first, lets `render` build the PDF with it, stores
/// the hash. `payload` is stored for traceability (these documents are not
/// re-rendered — the chart may have changed since).
pub async fn register_rendered<F>(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    kind: DocKind,
    patient_id: &str,
    payload: Value,
    render: F,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError>
where
    F: FnOnce(&str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError>,
{
    require_permission(session, kind.issue_permission())?;
    repo::ensure_numbering_authority(pool).await?;
    let issued_at = now_iso();
    let (doc, bytes) = immediate_tx!(pool, |conn| {
        let number = repo::next_number(conn, kind.prefix(), year_now()).await?;
        let bytes = render(&number)?;
        let doc = IssuedDocument {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),
            kind: kind.as_db().into(),
            document_number: number,
            source_table: Some("patient".into()),
            source_id: None,
            copy_variant: "ORIGINAL".into(),
            patient_id: Some(patient_id.to_string()),
            sha256: sha256_hex(&bytes),
            payload_json: payload.to_string(),
            status: "ISSUED".into(),
            cancels_id: None,
            issued_by: session.user_id.clone(),
            issued_at: issued_at.clone(),
            updated_at: issued_at.clone(),
        };
        repo::insert(conn, &doc).await?;
        Ok((doc, bytes))
    })?;
    after_insert(pool, session, &doc, "ISSUE_DOCUMENT").await;
    Ok(IssuedPdf {
        document_id: Some(doc.id),
        document_number: Some(doc.document_number),
        sha256: doc.sha256,
        pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
        reprint: false,
    })
}

// ===========================================================================
// Reprints / verification
// ===========================================================================

/// Render a stored document again with a "KOPIE" watermark.
pub async fn reprint(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    doc: &IssuedDocument,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let kind = DocKind::from_db(&doc.kind)
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::validation_code("error.documents.unknown_kind"))?;
    require_permission(session, kind.issue_permission())?;
    let logo = practice_logo(pool).await;
    let bytes = match kind {
        DocKind::Invoice | DocKind::Cancellation => {
            let mut inv: Invoice = serde_json::from_str(&doc.payload_json)
                .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("stored invoice: {e}")))?;
            inv.watermark = Some(WATERMARK_COPY.into());
            inv.logo = logo;
            render_goz_invoice(&inv)?
        }
        DocKind::Certificate | DocKind::Prescription | DocKind::Receipt => {
            let mut v: Value = serde_json::from_str(&doc.payload_json)
                .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("stored layout: {e}")))?;
            v["watermark"] = Value::String(WATERMARK_COPY.into());
            render_layout_value(&v, logo.as_ref())?
        }
        DocKind::DailyReport | DocKind::Report => {
            let mut input: ReportPdfInput = serde_json::from_str(&doc.payload_json)
                .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("stored report: {e}")))?;
            input.watermark = Some(WATERMARK_COPY.into());
            render_report_pdf(&input)?
        }
        DocKind::ChartExtract | DocKind::DischargeLeaflet => {
            // Not reproducible from stored data; issue a new extract instead.
            return Err(AppError::validation_code(
                "error.documents.reprint_not_supported",
            ));
        }
    };
    let sha = sha256_hex(&bytes);
    // The print row is the GoBD record; the audit entry is best-effort like
    // in `after_insert` (logged, never silently dropped).
    repo::record_reprint(pool, &doc.id, &sha, &session.user_id).await?;
    if let Err(e) = audit_repo::create(
        pool,
        &session.user_id,
        "REPRINT_DOCUMENT",
        &doc.kind,
        Some(&doc.id),
        Some(&format!("nr={} sha256={sha}", doc.document_number)),
    )
    .await
    {
        tracing::error!(event = "REPRINT_AUDIT_FAILED", error = %e);
    }
    Ok(IssuedPdf {
        document_id: Some(doc.id.clone()),
        document_number: Some(doc.document_number.clone()),
        sha256: sha,
        pdf_base64: STANDARD.encode(&bytes),
        reprint: true,
    })
}

pub async fn reprint_by_id(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    document_id: &str,
) -> Result<IssuedPdf, AppError> {
    let doc = repo::find_by_id(pool, document_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or(AppError::NotFound("Document".into()))?;
    reprint(pool, session, &doc).await
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn prefixes_are_unique() {
        let all = [
            DocKind::Invoice,
            DocKind::Cancellation,
            DocKind::Receipt,
            DocKind::Certificate,
            DocKind::Prescription,
            DocKind::ChartExtract,
            DocKind::DischargeLeaflet,
            DocKind::DailyReport,
            DocKind::Report,
        ];
        let mut p: Vec<_> = all.iter().map(|k| k.prefix()).collect();
        p.sort_unstable();
        p.dedup();
        assert_eq!(p.len(), all.len());
        for k in all {
            assert_eq!(DocKind::from_db(k.as_db()), Some(k));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_latin_payload_is_rejected() {
        let v = serde_json::json!({ "addressLines": ["محمد"] });
        assert!(check_renderable(&v).is_err());
        assert!(check_renderable(&serde_json::json!({ "a": ["Müller"] })).is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn server_fields_override_client_practice_and_strip_number() {
        let mut v = serde_json::json!({
            "kind": "certificate", "practiceLines": ["Fake Praxis"], "documentNumber": "X",
            "watermark": "", "logoKvJson": "{}", "documentTitle": "T"
        });
        let profile = PracticeProfile {
            name: "Echte Praxis".into(),
            addr: "Hauptstr. 1\n10115 Berlin".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        apply_server_fields(&mut v, &profile, "de", "employer").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["practiceLines"][0], "Echte Praxis");
        assert!(v.get("documentNumber").is_none());
        assert!(v.get("logoKvJson").is_none());
        assert_eq!(v["copyVariant"], "employer");
    }
}

//! Practice master data for official documents — read **only** from the
//! database (`app_kv` key `invoice.practice.v1`), never from the frontend.
//!
//! Legal identity fields (address, tax number, BSNR/ZANR, bank) are never
//! masked on official documents; the "privacy" toggles in Settings only apply
//! to screen previews.

use serde::Deserialize;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::database::app_kv_repo;
use crate::infrastructure::pdf::{footer_lines_for_practice, PdfLogo, PracticeFooter};

pub const PRACTICE_KV_KEY: &str = "invoice.practice.v1";
pub const DOCUMENT_LOCALE_KV_KEY: &str = "documents.locale.v1";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct PracticeProfile {
    pub name: String,
    pub addr: String,
    pub phone: Option<String>,
    pub fax: Option<String>,
    pub email: Option<String>,
    pub web: Option<String>,
    pub tax_number: Option<String>,
    pub vat_id: Option<String>,
    pub clinician_name: Option<String>,
    pub professional_title: Option<String>,
    pub zanr: Option<String>,
    pub bsnr: Option<String>,
    pub bank_iban: Option<String>,
    pub bank_bic: Option<String>,
    pub bank_name: Option<String>,
    pub account_holder: Option<String>,
    pub chamber: Option<String>,
    pub kzv: Option<String>,
    pub payment_terms_days: Option<u32>,
    pub vat_exemption_notice: Option<String>,
    pub emergency_phone: Option<String>,
}

fn opt(s: &Option<String>) -> Option<&str> {
    s.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
}

impl PracticeProfile {
    pub async fn load(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        let raw = app_kv_repo::get(pool, PRACTICE_KV_KEY)
            .await?
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let p: PracticeProfile = serde_json::from_str(&raw).unwrap_or_default();
        Ok(p)
    }

    /// Fails with a stable i18n code when fields required on every official
    /// document are missing (name, address, clinician).
    pub fn require_complete(&self) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        if self.name.trim().is_empty() || self.addr.trim().is_empty() {
            return Err(AppError::validation_code(
                "error.documents.practice_incomplete",
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn address_lines(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.addr
            .lines()
            .map(str::trim)
            .filter(|l| !l.is_empty())
            .map(String::from)
            .collect()
    }

    /// Letterhead lines: name, address, contact (no masking).
    pub fn letterhead_lines(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut out = vec![self.name.trim().to_string()];
        out.extend(self.address_lines());
        let contact: Vec<String> = [
            opt(&self.phone).map(|t| format!("Tel. {t}")),
            opt(&self.fax).map(|t| format!("Fax {t}")),
            opt(&self.email).map(String::from),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .collect();
        if !contact.is_empty() {
            out.push(contact.join(" · "));
        }
        if let Some(w) = opt(&self.web) {
            out.push(
                w.trim_start_matches("https://")
                    .trim_start_matches("http://")
                    .to_string(),
            );
        }
        let ids: Vec<String> = [
            opt(&self.bsnr).map(|b| format!("BSNR {b}")),
            opt(&self.zanr).map(|z| format!("ZANR {z}")),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .collect();
        if !ids.is_empty() {
            out.push(ids.join(" · "));
        }
        out
    }

    pub fn bank_lines(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut v = Vec::new();
        if let Some(h) = opt(&self.account_holder) {
            v.push(format!("Kontoinhaber: {h}"));
        }
        if let Some(b) = opt(&self.bank_name) {
            v.push(b.to_string());
        }
        if let Some(i) = opt(&self.bank_iban) {
            v.push(format!("IBAN {i}"));
        }
        if let Some(b) = opt(&self.bank_bic) {
            v.push(format!("BIC {b}"));
        }
        v
    }

    pub fn footer_lines(&self, locale: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        footer_lines_for_practice(
            &PracticeFooter {
                practice_name: format!(
                    "{} · {}",
                    self.name.trim(),
                    self.address_lines().join(", ")
                ),
                tax_number: opt(&self.tax_number).map(String::from),
                vat_id: opt(&self.vat_id).map(String::from),
                iban: opt(&self.bank_iban).map(String::from),
                bic: opt(&self.bank_bic).map(String::from),
                bank_name: opt(&self.bank_name).map(String::from),
                chamber: opt(&self.chamber).map(String::from),
            },
            locale,
        )
    }

    /// Signature block lines: name, title, ZANR/BSNR.
    pub fn signature_lines(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        let Some(name) = opt(&self.clinician_name) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        let mut v = vec![name.to_string()];
        if let Some(t) = opt(&self.professional_title) {
            v.push(t.to_string());
        }
        let ids: Vec<String> = [
            opt(&self.zanr).map(|z| format!("ZANR {z}")),
            opt(&self.bsnr).map(|b| format!("BSNR {b}")),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .collect();
        if !ids.is_empty() {
            v.push(ids.join(" · "));
        }
        v
    }

    pub fn payment_terms_text(&self) -> String {
        let days = self.payment_terms_days.unwrap_or(14).max(1);
        format!("Zahlbar innerhalb von {days} Tagen nach Rechnungsdatum ohne Abzug.")
    }

    pub fn vat_notice(&self) -> String {
        opt(&self.vat_exemption_notice)
            .map(String::from)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "Umsatzsteuerfrei gem. § 4 Nr. 14 Buchst. a UStG.".into())
    }
}

/// Document language chosen in Settings (default German).
pub async fn document_locale(pool: &SqlitePool) -> &'static str {
    let raw = app_kv_repo::get(pool, DOCUMENT_LOCALE_KV_KEY)
        .await
        .ok()
        .flatten()
        .unwrap_or_default();
    crate::infrastructure::pdf::pdf_locale(raw.trim().trim_matches('"'))
}

pub async fn practice_logo(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Option<PdfLogo> {
    match app_kv_repo::get(pool, "practice.logo.v1").await {
        Ok(Some(raw)) => PdfLogo::from_kv_json(&raw),
        _ => None,
    }
}

FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/src/application/documents/export_audit.rs

//! Audit trail for every file that leaves the encrypted database
//! (§ 630f BGB access traceability, Art. 30/32 GDPR).
//!
//! Called by the save commands themselves, so a UI path cannot skip it.

use serde::Deserialize;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use crate::application::auth_service::Session;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::infrastructure::database::{audit_repo, issued_document_repo::sha256_hex};

/// Context the UI sends with a save request.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ExportContext {
    /// e.g. `chart`, `receipt`, `patients_csv`, `report:statistics`, `audit_csv`
    pub kind: String,
    /// `pdf` | `csv` | `json` | `xml` | `zip`
    pub format: String,
    /// Main entity (patient id, payment id …) when there is one.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub entity_id: Option<String>,
    /// Registry number when the file is an issued document.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub document_number: Option<String>,
}

pub async fn log_export(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session: &Session,
    ctx: &ExportContext,
    destination: &str,
    bytes: &[u8],
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let details = format!(
        "kind={} format={} bytes={} sha256={} nr={} dest={}",
        ctx.kind,
        ctx.format,
        bytes.len(),
        sha256_hex(bytes),
        ctx.document_number.as_deref().unwrap_or("-"),
        // Only the file name — full paths can contain the user's name.
        std::path::Path::new(destination)
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("-"),
    );
    audit_repo::create(
        pool,
        &session.user_id,
        "EXPORT_FILE",
        &ctx.kind,
        ctx.entity_id.as_deref(),
        Some(&details),
    )
    .await
}

/// Neutralise spreadsheet formula injection (OWASP "CSV injection"):
/// cells starting with `= + - @` (or tab / CR) are prefixed with `'`.
pub fn csv_safe_cell(raw: &str) -> String {
    match raw.chars().next() {
        Some('=' | '+' | '-' | '@' | '\t' | '\r') => format!("'{raw}"),
        _ => raw.to_string(),
    }
}

/// RFC 4180 quoting + formula neutralisation for one cell.
pub fn csv_cell(raw: &str, delimiter: char) -> String {
    let v = csv_safe_cell(raw);
    if v.contains(delimiter) || v.contains('"') || v.contains('\n') || v.contains('\r') {
        format!("\"{}\"", v.replace('"', "\"\""))
    } else {
        v
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn formula_cells_are_neutralised() {
        assert_eq!(csv_safe_cell("=HYPERLINK(\"x\")"), "'=HYPERLINK(\"x\")");
        assert_eq!(csv_safe_cell("-12,50"), "'-12,50");
        assert_eq!(csv_safe_cell("Müller"), "Müller");
        assert_eq!(csv_cell("a;b", ';'), "\"a;b\"");
        assert_eq!(csv_cell("line\rbreak", ','), "\"line\rbreak\"");
    }
}

FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/src/infrastructure/database/repos/billing/invoice_document.rs

use crate::error::AppError;
use serde::Serialize;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, sqlx::FromRow)]
pub struct InvoiceDocumentRow {
    pub id: String,
    pub patient_id: String,
    pub document_number: String,
    pub payload_json: String,
    pub total_cents: i64,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub created_by: String,
}

const LIST_LIMIT_CAP: i64 = 500;

pub async fn list_recent(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    limit: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<InvoiceDocumentRow>, AppError> {
    let lim = limit.clamp(1, LIST_LIMIT_CAP);
    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, InvoiceDocumentRow>(
        "SELECT id, patient_id, document_number, payload_json, total_cents, created_at, created_by
         FROM invoice_document
         ORDER BY created_at DESC
         LIMIT ?1",
    )
    .bind(lim)
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await?;
    Ok(rows)
}

pub async fn insert_with_audit_trail(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    row: &InvoiceDocumentRow,
    audit_excerpt: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await?;

    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO invoice_document (
            id, patient_id, document_number, payload_json, total_cents, created_at, created_by
        ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7)",
    )
    .bind(&row.id)
    .bind(&row.patient_id)
    .bind(&row.document_number)
    .bind(&row.payload_json)
    .bind(row.total_cents)
    .bind(&row.created_at)
    .bind(&row.created_by)
    .execute(&mut *tx)
    .await?;

    let audit_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO invoice_document_audit (id, document_id, event, user_id, payload_excerpt)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, 'ISSUED', ?3, ?4)",
    )
    .bind(&audit_id)
    .bind(&row.id)
    .bind(&row.created_by)
    .bind(audit_excerpt)
    .execute(&mut *tx)
    .await?;

    crate::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::record_in_tx(
        &mut tx,
        "invoice_document",
        &row.id,
        "INSERT",
    )
    .await?;
    crate::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::record_in_tx(
        &mut tx,
        "invoice_document_audit",
        &audit_id,
        "INSERT",
    )
    .await?;

    tx.commit().await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn delete_for_patient(pool: &SqlitePool, patient_id: &str) -> Result<u64, AppError> {
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await?;
    let ids: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT id FROM invoice_document WHERE patient_id = ?1")
        .bind(patient_id)
        .fetch_all(&mut *tx)
        .await?;
    for id in &ids {
        crate::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::record_in_tx(
            &mut tx,
            "invoice_document",
            id,
            "DELETE",
        )
        .await?;
    }
    let n = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM invoice_document WHERE patient_id = ?1")
        .bind(patient_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?
        .rows_affected();
    tx.commit().await?;
    Ok(n)
}

import { issueInvoice, pdfBase64ToBytes } from "@/systems/practice-host/controllers/document.controller";

/** GOZ / GOÄ fee lines, § 9 expenses (material, external lab), other items. */
export type FeeSchedule = "goz" | "goae" | "material" | "lab" | "other";

export interface InvoiceLineInput {
    description: string;
    /** Net line amount in cents. The server recomputes fee lines from points × Punktwert × factor. */
    amount_cents: number;
    fee_schedule?: FeeSchedule;
    /** Points of the GOZ/GOÄ number — required for fee lines. */
    points?: number | null;
    /** § 6 Abs. 1 GOZ analog billing: number of the service the fee is taken from. */
    analog_of?: string | null;
    /** § 2 Abs. 1/2 GOZ agreement reference (factor > 3.5). */
    agreement_ref?: string | null;
    /** Receipt for an expense above 25,56 EUR is attached. */
    receipt_attached?: boolean;
    goz_nr?: string | null;
    factor?: number | null;
    unit_price_cents?: number | null;
    quantity?: number | null;
    tooth_nr?: string | null;
    treatment_date?: string | null;
    vat_percent?: number | null;
    material?: string | null;
    diagnosis_reason?: string | null;
}

export interface InvoiceInput {
    /** Set by the server when the invoice is issued — leave empty. */
    number: string;
    date: string;
    recipient_name: string;
    recipient_address: string[];
    practice_name: string;
    practice_address: string[];
    lines: InvoiceLineInput[];
    note?: string | null;
    clinician_name?: string | null;
    clinician_zanr?: string | null;
    practice_bsnr?: string | null;
    bank_details?: string[] | null;
    payment_terms_text?: string | null;
    vat_notice?: string | null;
    /** UI locale for letterhead chrome (`en`|`de`|`fr`|`ar`). */
    locale?: string | null;
    /** Arabic: logo on the top-right. */
    rtl?: boolean | null;
    /** Treated person — filled by the server from the patient record. */
    patient_name?: string;
    patient_birth_date?: string | null;
}

/*
 * allocateInvoiceDocumentNumber / allocateInvoiceNumber / allocateReceiptNumber /
 * allocateReportNumber were removed. Numbers are allocated by the server in the
 * same transaction that stores the document (issue_invoice, issue_clinical_document,
 * issue_report_pdf), so a cancelled dialog can no longer burn a number.
 */

/** Draft PDF (watermark "ENTWURF", no number). */
export async function renderInvoicePdf(patientId: string, invoice: InvoiceInput): Promise<Uint8Array> {
    const out = await issueInvoice(patientId, invoice, true);
    return pdfBase64ToBytes(out.pdfBase64);
}

FILE: packages/app/practice-host/src/controllers/invoice-document.controller.ts

import { practiceSystem } from "@/systems/practice-host/adapters/tauri-practice.adapter";
import type { InvoiceInput } from "@/systems/practice-host/controllers/invoice.controller";

export const INVOICE_HISTORY_MAX = 200;

export type SavedInvoice = {
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    patientId: string;
    invoice: InvoiceInput;
};

export type InvoiceDocumentListRow = {
    id: string;
    patient_id: string;
    document_number: string;
    payload_json: string;
    total_cents: number;
    created_at: string;
    created_by: string;
};

const LEGACY_LS_KEY = "medoc-invoice-history-v1";

function asStringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        return value.map((x) => (typeof x === "string" ? x : String(x ?? ""))).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    }
    if (typeof value === "string") {
        return value.split("\n").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    }
    return [];
}

function asOptionalString(value: unknown): string | null {
    return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value : null;
}

/** Fill missing fields from older SQLite / localStorage invoice payloads. */
export function normalizeInvoiceInput(
    raw: unknown,
    fallback: { number?: string; date?: string } = {},
): InvoiceInput {
    const o = raw != null && typeof raw === "object" ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
    const linesRaw = o.lines;
    const lines = Array.isArray(linesRaw)
        ? linesRaw
              .filter((x): x is Record<string, unknown> => x != null && typeof x === "object")
              .map((line) => ({
                  description: typeof line.description === "string" ? line.description : "",
                  amount_cents: typeof line.amount_cents === "number" && Number.isFinite(line.amount_cents) ? line.amount_cents : 0,
              }))
        : [];
    return {
        number: typeof o.number === "string" && o.number.trim() ? o.number : (fallback.number ?? ""),
        date: typeof o.date === "string" && o.date.trim() ? o.date : (fallback.date ?? ""),
        recipient_name: typeof o.recipient_name === "string" ? o.recipient_name : "",
        recipient_address: asStringArray(o.recipient_address),
        practice_name: typeof o.practice_name === "string" ? o.practice_name : "",
        practice_address: asStringArray(o.practice_address),
        lines,
        note: asOptionalString(o.note),
        clinician_name: asOptionalString(o.clinician_name),
        clinician_zanr: asOptionalString(o.clinician_zanr),
        practice_bsnr: asOptionalString(o.practice_bsnr),
        bank_details: Array.isArray(o.bank_details) ? asStringArray(o.bank_details) : o.bank_details == null ? null : asStringArray(o.bank_details),
        payment_terms_text: asOptionalString(o.payment_terms_text),
        vat_notice: asOptionalString(o.vat_notice),
        locale: asOptionalString(o.locale),
        rtl: typeof o.rtl === "boolean" ? o.rtl : null,
    };
}

function parseLegacy(raw: string | null): SavedInvoice[] {
    if (!raw) return [];
    try {
        const j = JSON.parse(raw) as unknown;
        if (!Array.isArray(j)) return [];
        const out: SavedInvoice[] = [];
        for (const x of j) {
            if (x == null || typeof x !== "object") continue;
            const row = x as Partial<SavedInvoice>;
            if (typeof row.id !== "string" || typeof row.createdAt !== "string" || typeof row.patientId !== "string") {
                continue;
            }
            out.push({
                id: row.id,
                createdAt: row.createdAt,
                patientId: row.patientId,
                invoice: normalizeInvoiceInput(row.invoice),
            });
        }
        return out;
    } catch {
        return [];
    }
}

export function sumInvoiceEur(inv: InvoiceInput): number {
    const cents = (inv.lines ?? []).reduce((s, l) => s + (l.amount_cents ?? 0), 0);
    return Math.round(cents) / 100;
}

export async function listInvoiceDocuments(limit?: number): Promise<SavedInvoice[]> {
    const lim = Math.min(limit ?? INVOICE_HISTORY_MAX, INVOICE_HISTORY_MAX);
    const rows = await practiceSystem.invoke<InvoiceDocumentListRow[]>("list_invoice_documents", {
        limit: lim,
    });
    return rows.map((r) => {
        let parsed: unknown = null;
        try {
            parsed = JSON.parse(r.payload_json) as unknown;
        } catch {
            parsed = null;
        }
        return {
            id: r.id,
            createdAt: r.created_at,
            patientId: r.patient_id,
            invoice: normalizeInvoiceInput(parsed, {
                number: r.document_number,
                date: r.created_at.slice(0, 10),
            }),
        };
    });
}

export async function appendInvoiceDocument(entry: SavedInvoice): Promise<void> {
    const totalCents = Math.round(sumInvoiceEur(entry.invoice) * 100);
    await practiceSystem.invoke<void>("append_invoice_document", {
        input: {
            id: entry.id,
            patient_id: entry.patientId,
            document_number: entry.invoice.number,
            payload_json: JSON.stringify(entry.invoice),
            total_cents: totalCents,
        },
    });
}

/** Import at most one batch from legacy localStorage (call from finance page on mount). */
export async function migrateLegacyInvoiceHistoryFromLocalStorageOnce(): Promise<void> {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || globalThis.localStorage == null) return;
    let raw: string | null = null;
    try {
        raw = localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_LS_KEY);
    } catch {
        return;
    }
    const entries = parseLegacy(raw);
    if (entries.length === 0) {
        try {
            localStorage.removeItem(LEGACY_LS_KEY);
        } catch {
            /* ignore */
        }
        return;
    }
    const existing = await listInvoiceDocuments(INVOICE_HISTORY_MAX);
    const existingIds = new Set(existing.map((e) => e.id));
    for (const e of entries) {
        if (!existingIds.has(e.id)) {
            try {
                await appendInvoiceDocument(e);
            } catch {
                /* if offline, keep LS */
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    try {
        localStorage.removeItem(LEGACY_LS_KEY);
    } catch {
        /* ignore */
    }
}

export function stripLegacyInvoiceHistoryLocalStorage(): void {
    if (globalThis.localStorage == null) return;
    try {
        localStorage.removeItem(LEGACY_LS_KEY);
    } catch {
        /* ignore */
    }
}

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

import { normalizeInvoiceInput, sumInvoiceEur } from "./invoice-document.controller";

        const inv = normalizeInvoiceInput(
            { number: "RE-1", date: "2026-09-29", recipient_name: "Ada" },
            { number: "fallback", date: "2020-01-01" },
        );

        expect(inv.practice_address).toEqual([]);
        expect(inv.lines).toEqual([]);

    });

        expect(inv.recipient_address).toEqual(["Street 1", "Berlin"]);
    });
});

FILE: packages/shared/src/lib/invoice-service-item.ts
