//! Dental invoice (Liquidation) in the structure of **Anlage 2 GOZ**.
//!
//! Columns: Datum | Region/Zahn | Nr. | Leistungsbeschreibung | Anz. | Faktor | Betrag EUR,
//! grouped into GOZ fees, GOÄ fees, § 9 expenses (material, external lab),
//! each with a subtotal; then net/VAT per rate (UStG § 14 Abs. 4 Nr. 7–8)
//! and the amount due. The document language is always German.
//!
//! The model is (de)serialisable so the issued invoice can be stored
//! verbatim in `issued_document.payload_json` and re-rendered later.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use super::core::{
    emit_pdf_document, format_date_dmy, format_eur, PageBuilder, PdfDocMeta, M_BOTTOM, M_LEFT,
    M_RIGHT,
};
use super::goz::{compute_totals, FACTOR_JUSTIFICATION_THRESHOLD};
use super::letterhead::{emit_letterhead, Letterhead, MetaRow};
use super::logo::PdfLogo;
use super::metrics::wrap_to_width;
use crate::error::AppError;

/// Which fee schedule / expense group a line belongs to.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum FeeSchedule {
    #[default]
    Goz,
    Goae,
    /// § 9 GOZ — practice material.
    Material,
    /// § 9 GOZ — external laboratory.
    Lab,
    /// Other non-fee items (e.g. Wegegeld § 8).
    Other,
}

impl FeeSchedule {
    pub fn is_fee(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, FeeSchedule::Goz | FeeSchedule::Goae)
    }

    fn heading(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            FeeSchedule::Goz => "Honorar nach GOZ",
            FeeSchedule::Goae => "Honorar nach GOÄ",
            FeeSchedule::Material => "Auslagen nach § 9 GOZ – Material",
            FeeSchedule::Lab => "Auslagen nach § 9 GOZ – Fremdlabor",
            FeeSchedule::Other => "Sonstige Positionen",
        }
    }

    const ORDER: [FeeSchedule; 5] = [
        FeeSchedule::Goz,
        FeeSchedule::Goae,
        FeeSchedule::Material,
        FeeSchedule::Lab,
        FeeSchedule::Other,
    ];
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum InvoiceKind {
    #[default]
    Invoice,
    /// Stornorechnung — negative amounts, references the original number.
    Cancellation,
}

/// One invoice line.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct InvoiceLine {
    /// Service description (understandable wording, § 10 Abs. 2 Nr. 2 GOZ).
    pub description: String,
    /// Line amount in cents (unit fee × quantity, net).
    pub amount_cents: i64,
    /// Fee number ("0010", "2197", GOÄ "Ä1").
    pub goz_nr: Option<String>,
    /// Increase factor.
    pub factor: Option<f64>,
    /// Unit price in cents (informational).
    pub unit_price_cents: Option<i64>,
    pub quantity: Option<i32>,
    /// Tooth / region (FDI).
    pub tooth_nr: Option<String>,
    /// ISO date of the service.
    pub treatment_date: Option<String>,
    /// VAT rate in percent (0 = exempt).
    pub vat_percent: Option<f64>,
    /// Kind of material (shown as sub-line).
    pub material: Option<String>,
    /// Justification for factor > 2.3 (§ 10 Abs. 3 GOZ).
    pub diagnosis_reason: Option<String>,
    pub fee_schedule: FeeSchedule,
    /// Points of the fee number — used to recompute the amount.
    pub points: Option<u32>,
    /// Analog billing (§ 6 Abs. 1 GOZ): number of the service the fee is
    /// taken from. `goz_nr` then holds the analog number.
    pub analog_of: Option<String>,
    /// Reference to an agreement under § 2 Abs. 1 und 2 GOZ (factor > 3.5).
    pub agreement_ref: Option<String>,
    /// Receipt for expenses above 25,56 EUR is attached.
    pub receipt_attached: bool,
}

impl InvoiceLine {
    /// Construct a minimal line (all GOZ fields empty).
    pub fn simple(description: impl Into<String>, amount_cents: i64) -> Self {
        Self {
            description: description.into(),
            amount_cents,
            fee_schedule: FeeSchedule::Other,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }
}

/// Complete invoice (UStG § 14 Abs. 4 + GOZ § 10).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct Invoice {
    pub kind: InvoiceKind,
    pub number: String,
    /// ISO issue date.
    pub date: String,
    pub recipient_name: String,
    pub recipient_address: Vec<String>,
    /// Treated person (may differ from the recipient, e.g. a child).
    pub patient_name: String,
    /// ISO date of birth of the treated person.
    pub patient_birth_date: Option<String>,
    pub practice_name: String,
    pub practice_address: Vec<String>,
    /// Full letterhead (name, address, contact, BSNR/ZANR), filled by the
    /// server. When empty, it is built from name + address + ids.
    pub practice_lines: Vec<String>,
    /// DIN footer (identity, chamber, tax no., bank), filled by the server.
    pub footer_lines: Vec<String>,
    pub lines: Vec<InvoiceLine>,
    pub note: Option<String>,
    pub clinician_name: Option<String>,
    pub clinician_zanr: Option<String>,
    pub practice_bsnr: Option<String>,
    /// Steuernummer.
    pub tax_number: Option<String>,
    /// USt-IdNr.
    pub vat_id: Option<String>,
    pub bank_details: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub payment_terms_text: Option<String>,
    /// Exemption notice, e.g. "Umsatzsteuerfrei gem. § 4 Nr. 14 UStG".
    pub vat_notice: Option<String>,
    /// Original invoice number (cancellations).
    pub cancels_number: Option<String>,
    /// Original invoice date (cancellations).
    pub cancels_date: Option<String>,
    /// "ENTWURF" (preview) / "KOPIE" (reprint) — never stored.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub watermark: Option<String>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub logo: Option<PdfLogo>,
    /// Kept for API compatibility; GOZ invoices are always German.
    pub locale: String,
    pub rtl: bool,
}

impl Invoice {
    /// Gross total (net + VAT) in cents.
    pub fn total_cents(&self) -> i64 {
        compute_totals(self).gross
    }

    fn service_period(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let mut dates: Vec<&str> = self
            .lines
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|l| l.treatment_date.as_deref())
            .filter(|d| d.len() >= 10)
            .map(|d| &d[..10])
            .collect();
        dates.sort_unstable();
        let first = *dates.first()?;
        let last = *dates.last()?;
        Some(if first == last {
            format_date_dmy(first)
        } else {
            format!("{} – {}", format_date_dmy(first), format_date_dmy(last))
        })
    }
}

/// "12,94" (German decimal comma, thousands dot, no currency).
pub fn fmt_amount(cents: i64) -> String {
    format_eur(cents).trim_end_matches(" EUR").to_string()
}

fn fmt_factor(f: f64) -> String {
    let s = if ((f * 100.0).round() as i64) % 10 == 0 {
        format!("{f:.1}")
    } else {
        format!("{f:.2}")
    };
    s.replace('.', ",")
}

// Column layout relative to M_LEFT (content width 467 pt).
const X_DATE: i32 = M_LEFT;
const X_REGION: i32 = M_LEFT + 54;
const X_NR: i32 = M_LEFT + 88;
const X_DESC: i32 = M_LEFT + 130;
const DESC_W: i32 = 186;
const X_QTY_R: i32 = M_LEFT + 346;
const X_FACTOR_R: i32 = M_LEFT + 390;
const X_AMOUNT_R: i32 = M_RIGHT;
const ROW_FS: i32 = 8;
const ROW_STEP: i32 = 10;

fn emit_table_header(pb: &mut PageBuilder) {
    pb.table_header_band(M_LEFT, pb.y + 2, M_RIGHT - M_LEFT);
    pb.text(X_DATE + 2, 8, true, "Datum");
    pb.text(X_REGION, 8, true, "Region");
    pb.text(X_NR, 8, true, "Nr.");
    pb.text(X_DESC, 8, true, "Leistungsbeschreibung");
    pb.text_right(X_QTY_R, 8, true, "Anz.");
    pb.text_right(X_FACTOR_R, 8, true, "Faktor");
    pb.text_right(X_AMOUNT_R - 2, 8, true, "Betrag EUR");
    pb.advance(10);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(11);
}

/// All text lines of the description column for one invoice line.
fn description_lines(line: &InvoiceLine) -> Vec<(String, bool)> {
    let w = f64::from(DESC_W);
    let mut out: Vec<(String, bool)> = wrap_to_width(&line.description, ROW_FS, false, w)
        .into_iter()
        .map(|s| (s, false))
        .collect();
    let mut sub = |text: String| {
        for s in wrap_to_width(&text, 7, false, w) {
            out.push((s, true));
        }
    };
    if let Some(orig) = line.analog_of.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
        sub(format!("entsprechend Nr. {orig} gem. § 6 Abs. 1 GOZ"));
    }
    if let Some(m) = line.material.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
        sub(format!("Art der Auslage: {m}"));
    }
    if line.receipt_attached {
        sub("Beleg liegt bei.".into());
    }
    if let Some(reason) = line
        .diagnosis_reason
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        let label = if line.factor.unwrap_or(0.0) > FACTOR_JUSTIFICATION_THRESHOLD {
            "Begründung (§ 10 Abs. 3 GOZ)"
        } else {
            "Hinweis"
        };
        sub(format!("{label}: {reason}"));
    }
    if let Some(a) = line
        .agreement_ref
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        sub(format!("Vereinbarung gem. § 2 Abs. 1 und 2 GOZ: {a}"));
    }
    out
}

fn emit_line(pb: &mut PageBuilder, line: &InvoiceLine) {
    let desc = description_lines(line);
    let height = desc.len() as i32 * ROW_STEP + 4;
    // Whole row must fit — otherwise new page with repeated header.
    if pb.y - height < M_BOTTOM + 6 {
        pb.break_page();
        emit_table_header(pb);
    }
    let top = pb.y;
    let date = line
        .treatment_date
        .as_deref()
        .map(format_date_dmy)
        .unwrap_or_default();
    pb.text(X_DATE + 2, ROW_FS, false, &date);
    pb.text(
        X_REGION,
        ROW_FS,
        false,
        line.tooth_nr.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
    );
    let nr = match (&line.goz_nr, line.analog_of.is_some()) {
        (Some(n), true) => format!("{n}a"),
        (Some(n), false) => n.clone(),
        (None, _) => String::new(),
    };
    pb.text(X_NR, ROW_FS, false, &nr);
    if line.fee_schedule.is_fee() {
        pb.text_right(
            X_QTY_R,
            ROW_FS,
            false,
            &line.quantity.unwrap_or(1).to_string(),
        );
        if let Some(f) = line.factor {
            pb.text_right(X_FACTOR_R, ROW_FS, false, &fmt_factor(f));
        }
    }
    pb.text_right(
        X_AMOUNT_R - 2,
        ROW_FS,
        false,
        &fmt_amount(line.amount_cents),
    );
    for (i, (text, small)) in desc.iter().enumerate() {
        pb.y = top - i as i32 * ROW_STEP;
        pb.text(X_DESC, if *small { 7 } else { ROW_FS }, false, text);
    }
    pb.y = top - height;
}

fn emit_sum_row(pb: &mut PageBuilder, label: &str, cents: i64, bold: bool, rule: bool) {
    pb.ensure_space(20);
    if rule {
        pb.hline(M_LEFT + 250, M_RIGHT);
        pb.advance(11);
    }
    pb.text_right(X_FACTOR_R, 9, bold, label);
    pb.text_right(X_AMOUNT_R - 2, 9, bold, &fmt_amount(cents));
    pb.advance(13);
}

/// Render an invoice (or cancellation invoice). Call [`super::goz::validate_invoice`]
/// first — the issuing service does this before allocating the number.
pub fn render_goz_invoice(inv: &Invoice) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let cancellation = inv.kind == InvoiceKind::Cancellation;
    let totals = compute_totals(inv);
    let mut pb = PageBuilder::new();
    pb.watermark = inv.watermark.clone();

    // ---- Letterhead -------------------------------------------------------
    let mut practice = if inv.practice_lines.is_empty() {
        vec![inv.practice_name.clone()]
    } else {
        inv.practice_lines.clone()
    };
    if inv.practice_lines.is_empty() {
        practice.extend(inv.practice_address.iter().cloned());
        let ids: Vec<String> = [
            inv.practice_bsnr.as_deref().map(|b| format!("BSNR {b}")),
            inv.clinician_zanr.as_deref().map(|z| format!("ZANR {z}")),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .filter(|s| s.len() > 5)
        .collect();
        if !ids.is_empty() {
            practice.push(ids.join(" · "));
        }
    }

    let mut meta = vec![
        MetaRow::new(
            if cancellation {
                "Storno-Nr."
            } else {
                "Rechnungs-Nr."
            },
            &inv.number,
        ),
        MetaRow::new("Rechnungsdatum", format_date_dmy(&inv.date)),
    ];
    if let Some(p) = inv.service_period() {
        meta.push(MetaRow::new("Leistungszeitraum", p));
    }
    if !inv.patient_name.trim().is_empty() {
        let dob = inv
            .patient_birth_date
            .as_deref()
            .map(|d| format!(", geb. {}", format_date_dmy(d)))
            .unwrap_or_default();
        meta.push(MetaRow::new(
            "Behandelte Person",
            format!("{}{dob}", inv.patient_name),
        ));
    }
    if let Some(c) = inv
        .clinician_name
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        meta.push(MetaRow::new("Behandler/in", c));
    }

    let mut recipient = vec![inv.recipient_name.clone()];
    recipient.extend(inv.recipient_address.iter().cloned());

    let lh = Letterhead {
        practice_lines: &practice,
        meta_rows: &meta,
        address_lines: &recipient,
        show_sender_hint: true,
        logo: inv.logo.as_ref(),
        rtl: false,
        locale: "de",
        ..Default::default()
    };
    emit_letterhead(&mut pb, &lh);

    // DIN footer: identity, tax, bank on every page.
    let mut footer = if !inv.footer_lines.is_empty() {
        inv.footer_lines.clone()
    } else {
        Vec::new()
    };
    if footer.is_empty() {
        footer.push(format!(
            "{} · {}",
            inv.practice_name,
            inv.practice_address.join(", ")
        ));
        let tax: Vec<String> = [
            inv.tax_number.as_deref().map(|t| format!("Steuer-Nr. {t}")),
            inv.vat_id.as_deref().map(|v| format!("USt-IdNr. {v}")),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .flatten()
        .collect();
        if !tax.is_empty() {
            footer.push(tax.join(" · "));
        }
        if let Some(bank) = inv.bank_details.as_ref().filter(|b| !b.is_empty()) {
            footer.push(bank.join(" · "));
        }
    }
    pb.footer_lines.extend(footer);

    let title = if cancellation {
        "Stornorechnung"
    } else {
        "Rechnung"
    };
    pb.set_continuation(&inv.practice_name, &format!("{title} {}", inv.number), "de");

    // ---- Title --------------------------------------------------------------
    pb.text(M_LEFT, 16, true, title);
    pb.advance(18);
    let has_goz = inv.lines.iter().any(|l| l.fee_schedule == FeeSchedule::Goz);
    let has_goae = inv
        .lines
        .iter()
        .any(|l| l.fee_schedule == FeeSchedule::Goae);
    let basis = match (has_goz, has_goae) {
        (true, true) => "Liquidation nach GOZ und GOÄ",
        (true, false) => "Liquidation nach der Gebührenordnung für Zahnärzte (GOZ)",
        (false, true) => "Liquidation nach der Gebührenordnung für Ärzte (GOÄ)",
        _ => "",
    };
    if !basis.is_empty() {
        pb.text(M_LEFT, 9, false, basis);
        pb.advance(14);
    }
    if cancellation {
        let orig = inv.cancels_number.as_deref().unwrap_or("-");
        let date = inv
            .cancels_date
            .as_deref()
            .map(|d| format!(" vom {}", format_date_dmy(d)))
            .unwrap_or_default();
        pb.wrapped(
            M_LEFT,
            9,
            false,
            &format!("Diese Stornorechnung hebt die Rechnung Nr. {orig}{date} vollständig auf."),
            M_RIGHT - M_LEFT,
        );
    }
    pb.advance(4);

    // ---- Table, grouped by schedule ---------------------------------------
    emit_table_header(&mut pb);
    for schedule in FeeSchedule::ORDER {
        let lines: Vec<&InvoiceLine> = inv
            .lines
            .iter()
            .filter(|l| l.fee_schedule == schedule)
            .collect();
        if lines.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        pb.ensure_space(30);
        pb.text(M_LEFT, 8, true, schedule.heading());
        pb.advance(11);
        let mut sum = 0;
        for l in lines {
            emit_line(&mut pb, l);
            sum += l.amount_cents;
        }
        emit_sum_row(
            &mut pb,
            &format!("Zwischensumme {}", short_label(schedule)),
            sum,
            false,
            true,
        );
        pb.advance(4);
    }

    // ---- Totals --------------------------------------------------------------
    pb.ensure_space(70);
    pb.advance(4);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT + 250, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(3);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT + 250, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(13);
    let total_label = if cancellation { "Stornobetrag" } else { "Rechnungsbetrag" };
    if totals.vat_groups.is_empty() {
        emit_sum_row(&mut pb, total_label, totals.gross, true, false);
    } else {
        emit_sum_row(&mut pb, "Nettobetrag", totals.net, false, false);
        for (rate, net, tax) in &totals.vat_groups {
            let rate_s = fmt_factor(*rate as f64 / 100.0)
                .trim_end_matches(",0")
                .to_string();
            emit_sum_row(
                &mut pb,
                &format!("zzgl. {rate_s} % USt auf {}", fmt_amount(*net)),
                *tax,
                false,
                false,
            );
        }
        emit_sum_row(&mut pb, total_label, totals.gross, true, true);
    }

    // ---- Notices -------------------------------------------------------------
    pb.advance(6);
    let width = M_RIGHT - M_LEFT;
    let exempt_part = inv
        .lines
        .iter()
        .any(|l| l.vat_percent.unwrap_or(0.0) <= 0.0);
    if exempt_part {
        if let Some(n) = inv.vat_notice.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
            let text = if totals.vat_groups.is_empty() {
                n.to_string()
            } else {
                format!("Positionen ohne USt-Ausweis: {n}")
            };
            pb.wrapped(M_LEFT, 9, false, &text, width);
        }
    }
    if !cancellation {
        if let Some(t) = inv
            .payment_terms_text
            .as_deref()
            .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
        {
            pb.wrapped(M_LEFT, 9, false, t, width);
        }
        // Payment details in the body, not only in the footer, so the
        // patient can transfer without searching the small print.
        if let Some(bank) = inv.bank_details.as_ref().filter(|b| !b.is_empty()) {
            pb.advance(4);
            pb.wrapped(
                M_LEFT,
                9,
                false,
                &format!(
                    "Bitte überweisen Sie den Rechnungsbetrag unter Angabe der Rechnungsnummer {} auf folgendes Konto:",
                    inv.number
                ),
                width,
            );
            // Holder + bank on one line, IBAN + BIC on the next.
            let (head, tail): (Vec<&String>, Vec<&String>) =
                bank.iter().partition(|l| !l.starts_with("IBAN") && !l.starts_with("BIC"));
            for part in [head, tail] {
                if !part.is_empty() {
                    let line: Vec<&str> = part.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();
                    pb.wrapped(M_LEFT, 9, true, &line.join(" · "), width);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(n) = inv.note.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
        pb.advance(4);
        pb.wrapped(M_LEFT, 9, false, n, width);
    }

    let pages = pb.finish();
    let images: Vec<PdfLogo> = inv.logo.clone().into_iter().collect();
    emit_pdf_document(
        &pages,
        &PdfDocMeta {
            title: format!("{title} {}", inv.number),
            subject: Some(inv.number.clone()),
            author: Some(inv.practice_name.clone()),
            locale: "de".into(),
        },
        &images,
    )
}

fn short_label(s: FeeSchedule) -> &'static str {
    match s {
        FeeSchedule::Goz => "GOZ",
        FeeSchedule::Goae => "GOÄ",
        FeeSchedule::Material => "Material",
        FeeSchedule::Lab => "Fremdlabor",
        FeeSchedule::Other => "Sonstiges",
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::super::goz::{unit_fee_cents, validate_invoice};
    use super::*;

    pub fn sample() -> Invoice {
        Invoice {
            number: "RE-2026-000001".into(),
            date: "2026-06-02".into(),
            recipient_name: "Max Muster".into(),
            recipient_address: vec!["Musterweg 1".into(), "10115 Berlin".into()],
            patient_name: "Lena Muster".into(),
            patient_birth_date: Some("2014-03-01".into()),
            practice_name: "Zahnarztpraxis Dr. Beispiel".into(),
            practice_address: vec!["Hauptstraße 2".into(), "10115 Berlin".into()],
            clinician_name: Some("Dr. Maria Beispiel".into()),
            practice_bsnr: Some("123456789".into()),
            clinician_zanr: Some("987654321".into()),
            tax_number: Some("12/345/67890".into()),
            bank_details: Some(vec![
                "IBAN DE89 3704 0044 0532 0130 00".into(),
                "BIC COBADEFFXXX".into(),
            ]),
            vat_notice: Some("Umsatzsteuerfrei gem. § 4 Nr. 14 UStG.".into()),
            payment_terms_text: Some("Zahlbar innerhalb von 14 Tagen ohne Abzug.".into()),
            lines: vec![
                InvoiceLine {
                    description: "Eingehende Untersuchung".into(),
                    goz_nr: Some("0010".into()),
                    points: Some(100),
                    factor: Some(2.3),
                    amount_cents: unit_fee_cents(FeeSchedule::Goz, 100, 2.3),
                    treatment_date: Some("2026-05-20".into()),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
                InvoiceLine {
                    description: "Testposition Füllung, dreiflächig".into(),
                    goz_nr: Some("9999".into()),
                    points: Some(1_000),
                    factor: Some(3.0),
                    amount_cents: unit_fee_cents(FeeSchedule::Goz, 1_000, 3.0),
                    tooth_nr: Some("36".into()),
                    treatment_date: Some("2026-05-27".into()),
                    diagnosis_reason: Some(
                        "Erschwerte Trockenlegung bei subgingivaler Ausdehnung".into(),
                    ),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
                InvoiceLine {
                    description: "Komposit-Material".into(),
                    fee_schedule: FeeSchedule::Material,
                    amount_cents: 3_200,
                    material: Some("Komposit, Charge 4711".into()),
                    receipt_attached: true,
                    treatment_date: Some("2026-05-27".into()),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            ],
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn valid_sample_passes_and_renders_german_form() {
        let inv = sample();
        validate_invoice(&inv).expect("valid");
        let pdf = render_goz_invoice(&inv).unwrap();
        let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&pdf);
        for needle in [
            "Rechnung",
            "Leistungsbeschreibung",
            "Zwischensumme GOZ",
            "Rechnungsbetrag",
            "Behandelte Person",
            "Steuer-Nr. 12/345/67890",
            "Begr\\374ndung",
            "Leistungszeitraum",
        ] {
            assert!(s.contains(needle), "missing {needle}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_factor_above_2_3_without_reason_and_wrong_amount() {
        let mut inv = sample();
        inv.lines[1].diagnosis_reason = None;
        inv.lines[0].amount_cents += 50;
        let err = validate_invoice(&inv).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(err.contains("Begründung"));
        assert!(err.contains("passt nicht"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_missing_patient_dob_and_service_date() {
        let mut inv = sample();
        inv.patient_birth_date = None;
        inv.lines[0].treatment_date = None;
        let err = validate_invoice(&inv).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(err.contains("Geburtsdatum"));
        assert!(err.contains("Leistungserbringung"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn taxable_line_needs_tax_id_and_shows_vat() {
        let mut inv = sample();
        inv.tax_number = None;
        inv.lines[2].vat_percent = Some(19.0);
        assert!(validate_invoice(&inv)
            .unwrap_err()
            .to_string()
            .contains("Steuernummer"));
        inv.tax_number = Some("12/345/67890".into());
        validate_invoice(&inv).unwrap();
        let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&render_goz_invoice(&inv).unwrap()).into_owned();
        assert!(s.contains("zzgl. 19 % USt"));
        assert_eq!(inv.total_cents(), compute_totals(&inv).net + 608);
    }

    #[test]
    fn cancellation_references_original() {
        let mut inv = sample();
        inv.kind = InvoiceKind::Cancellation;
        inv.number = "ST-2026-000001".into();
        inv.cancels_number = Some("RE-2026-000001".into());
        for l in &mut inv.lines {
            l.amount_cents = -l.amount_cents;
        }
        validate_invoice(&inv).unwrap();
        let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&render_goz_invoice(&inv).unwrap()).into_owned();
        assert!(s.contains("Stornorechnung"));
        assert!(s.contains("RE-2026-000001"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn long_invoice_repeats_header_on_every_page() {
        let mut inv = sample();
        let base = inv.lines[0].clone();
        inv.lines = (0..80).map(|_| base.clone()).collect();
        let s = String::from_utf8_lossy(&render_goz_invoice(&inv).unwrap()).into_owned();
        let pages = s.matches("/Type /Page ").count();
        assert!(pages >= 3);
        assert_eq!(s.matches("(Leistungsbeschreibung)").count(), pages);
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/src/infrastructure/pdf/goz.rs
================================================================================
//! GOZ / GOÄ fee calculation and invoice validation (GOZ §§ 2, 5, 6, 9, 10;
//! UStG § 14 (4)).
//!
//! Every issued invoice goes through [`validate_invoice`] **on the server**.
//! Amounts sent by the frontend are recomputed from points × Punktwert ×
//! factor and rejected when they disagree, so a manipulated DTO cannot
//! produce an "official" invoice with wrong fees.

use super::core::is_winansi_renderable;
use super::invoice_goz::{FeeSchedule, Invoice, InvoiceKind, InvoiceLine};
use crate::error::AppError;

/// GOZ Punktwert in 1/100000 cent (5,62421 Cent, § 5 Abs. 1 GOZ).
pub const GOZ_PUNKTWERT_E5: i64 = 562_421;
/// GOÄ Punktwert in 1/100000 cent (5,82873 Cent, § 5 Abs. 1 GOÄ).
/// Update here when the GOÄ reform takes effect.
pub const GOAE_PUNKTWERT_E5: i64 = 582_873;

/// Threshold factor that requires a written justification (§ 5 Abs. 2 / § 10 Abs. 3 GOZ).
pub const FACTOR_JUSTIFICATION_THRESHOLD: f64 = 2.3;
/// Highest factor without a § 2 agreement.
pub const FACTOR_MAX_WITHOUT_AGREEMENT: f64 = 3.5;
/// Single expense above which a receipt must be attached (§ 10 Abs. 2 Nr. 5 GOZ).
pub const EXPENSE_RECEIPT_THRESHOLD_CENTS: i64 = 2_556;

/// Fee for one unit in cents: points × Punktwert × factor, rounded half-up.
pub fn unit_fee_cents(schedule: FeeSchedule, points: u32, factor: f64) -> i64 {
    let pw = match schedule {
        FeeSchedule::Goae => GOAE_PUNKTWERT_E5,
        _ => GOZ_PUNKTWERT_E5,
    };
    // Factor with up to 2 decimals → integer hundredths to avoid f64 drift.
    let factor_x100 = (factor * 100.0).round() as i64;
    let raw = i64::from(points) * pw * factor_x100; // cents × 1e5 × 100
    let denom = 100_000 * 100;
    (raw + denom / 2) / denom
}

/// Expected line amount (unit fee × quantity) for fee lines.
pub fn expected_line_cents(line: &InvoiceLine) -> Option<i64> {
    if !line.fee_schedule.is_fee() {
        return None;
    }
    let points = line.points?;
    let factor = line.factor?;
    let qty = i64::from(line.quantity.unwrap_or(1).max(1));
    Some(unit_fee_cents(line.fee_schedule, points, factor) * qty)
}

/// Totals per VAT rate (net, tax) plus grand total — all in cents.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct InvoiceTotals {
    pub fees_goz: i64,
    pub fees_goae: i64,
    pub material: i64,
    pub lab: i64,
    pub other: i64,
    /// (rate in percent × 100, net cents, tax cents) for every rate > 0.
    pub vat_groups: Vec<(i64, i64, i64)>,
    pub net: i64,
    pub tax: i64,
    pub gross: i64,
}

pub fn compute_totals(inv: &Invoice) -> InvoiceTotals {
    let mut t = InvoiceTotals::default();
    let mut groups: std::collections::BTreeMap<i64, i64> = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    for l in &inv.lines {
        match l.fee_schedule {
            FeeSchedule::Goz => t.fees_goz += l.amount_cents,
            FeeSchedule::Goae => t.fees_goae += l.amount_cents,
            FeeSchedule::Material => t.material += l.amount_cents,
            FeeSchedule::Lab => t.lab += l.amount_cents,
            FeeSchedule::Other => t.other += l.amount_cents,
        }
        t.net += l.amount_cents;
        let rate = (l.vat_percent.unwrap_or(0.0) * 100.0).round() as i64;
        if rate > 0 {
            *groups.entry(rate).or_default() += l.amount_cents;
        }
    }
    for (rate, net) in groups {
        // Tax per rate, rounded half away from zero (works for cancellations too).
        let tax = (net * rate + net.signum() * 5_000) / 10_000;
        t.tax += tax;
        t.vat_groups.push((rate, net, tax));
    }
    t.gross = t.net + t.tax;
    t
}

fn is_iso_date(s: &str) -> bool {
    chrono::NaiveDate::parse_from_str(s.get(..10).unwrap_or(""), "%Y-%m-%d").is_ok()
}

fn blank(s: &Option<String>) -> bool {
    s.as_deref().map(|x| x.trim().is_empty()).unwrap_or(true)
}

/// Validate an invoice before it is numbered, stored and rendered.
/// Returns all problems at once (German, shown to the user).
pub fn validate_invoice(inv: &Invoice) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let mut errs: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let cancellation = inv.kind == InvoiceKind::Cancellation;

    if inv.number.trim().is_empty() {
        errs.push("Rechnungsnummer fehlt.".into());
    }
    if !is_iso_date(&inv.date) {
        errs.push("Rechnungsdatum fehlt oder ist ungültig (JJJJ-MM-TT).".into());
    }
    if inv.practice_name.trim().is_empty() || inv.practice_address.is_empty() {
        errs.push("Name und Anschrift der Praxis fehlen (§ 14 Abs. 4 Nr. 1 UStG).".into());
    }
    if inv.recipient_name.trim().is_empty() || inv.recipient_address.is_empty() {
        errs.push("Name und Anschrift des Rechnungsempfängers fehlen.".into());
    }
    if inv.lines.is_empty() {
        errs.push("Die Rechnung enthält keine Positionen.".into());
    }
    let has_fees = inv.lines.iter().any(|l| l.fee_schedule.is_fee());
    if has_fees && (inv.patient_name.trim().is_empty() || blank(&inv.patient_birth_date)) {
        errs.push("Name und Geburtsdatum der behandelten Person fehlen.".into());
    }
    if cancellation && blank(&inv.cancels_number) {
        errs.push("Stornorechnung ohne Bezug auf die ursprüngliche Rechnungsnummer.".into());
    }

    for (i, l) in inv.lines.iter().enumerate() {
        let pos = i + 1;
        if l.description.trim().is_empty() {
            errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Leistungsbeschreibung fehlt."));
        }
        if blank(&l.treatment_date) || !is_iso_date(l.treatment_date.as_deref().unwrap_or("")) {
            errs.push(format!(
                "Pos. {pos}: Datum der Leistungserbringung fehlt (§ 10 Abs. 2 Nr. 1 GOZ)."
            ));
        }
        if !cancellation && l.amount_cents <= 0 {
            errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Betrag muss größer als 0 sein."));
        }
        if cancellation && l.amount_cents >= 0 {
            errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Stornopositionen müssen negativ sein."));
        }

        if l.fee_schedule.is_fee() {
            if blank(&l.goz_nr) {
                errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Gebührennummer fehlt."));
            }
            match l.factor {
                None => errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Steigerungsfaktor fehlt.")),
                Some(f) if f < 1.0 => errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Faktor unter 1,0 ist unzulässig.")),
                Some(f) if f > FACTOR_MAX_WITHOUT_AGREEMENT && blank(&l.agreement_ref) => errs.push(format!(
                    "Pos. {pos}: Faktor {f:.1} über 3,5 nur mit Vereinbarung nach § 2 Abs. 1 und 2 GOZ."
                )),
                Some(f) if f > FACTOR_JUSTIFICATION_THRESHOLD && blank(&l.diagnosis_reason) => errs.push(format!(
                    "Pos. {pos}: Faktor {f:.1} über 2,3 erfordert eine schriftliche Begründung (§ 10 Abs. 3 GOZ)."
                )),
                _ => {}
            }
            match (l.points, expected_line_cents(l)) {
                (None, _) => errs.push(format!("Pos. {pos}: Punktzahl fehlt.")),
                (Some(_), Some(expected)) => {
                    if (l.amount_cents.abs() - expected).abs() > 1 {
                        errs.push(format!(
                            "Pos. {pos}: Betrag {} Cent passt nicht zu Punktzahl × Punktwert × Faktor ({} Cent).",
                            l.amount_cents, expected
                        ));
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
            if l.analog_of.is_some() && l.fee_schedule != FeeSchedule::Goz {
                errs.push(format!(
                    "Pos. {pos}: Analogberechnung nach § 6 Abs. 1 nur für GOZ-Positionen."
                ));
            }
        } else if matches!(l.fee_schedule, FeeSchedule::Material | FeeSchedule::Lab)
            && l.amount_cents.abs() > EXPENSE_RECEIPT_THRESHOLD_CENTS
            && !l.receipt_attached
        {
            errs.push(format!(
                "Pos. {pos}: Auslage über 25,56 EUR – Beleg muss beigefügt werden (§ 10 Abs. 2 Nr. 5 GOZ)."
            ));
        }
    }

    let taxable = inv.lines.iter().any(|l| l.vat_percent.unwrap_or(0.0) > 0.0);
    if taxable && blank(&inv.tax_number) && blank(&inv.vat_id) {
        errs.push("Umsatzsteuerpflichtige Positionen: Steuernummer oder USt-IdNr. fehlt (§ 14 Abs. 4 Nr. 2 UStG).".into());
    }
    if !taxable && blank(&inv.vat_notice) {
        errs.push("Hinweis auf die Steuerbefreiung fehlt (z. B. § 4 Nr. 14 UStG).".into());
    }

    // Everything printed must be representable in the PDF fonts.
    let mut texts: Vec<&str> = vec![&inv.recipient_name, &inv.practice_name, &inv.patient_name];
    texts.extend(inv.recipient_address.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()));
    texts.extend(inv.practice_address.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()));
    texts.extend(inv.lines.iter().map(|l| l.description.as_str()));
    texts.extend(
        inv.lines
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|l| l.diagnosis_reason.as_deref()),
    );
    if texts.iter().any(|t| !is_winansi_renderable(t)) {
        errs.push(
            "Text enthält Zeichen, die im PDF nicht darstellbar sind (nur lateinische Schrift)."
                .into(),
        );
    }

    if errs.is_empty() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(AppError::Validation(errs.join("\n")))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn goz_0010_at_2_3_is_12_94() {
        // GOZ 0010 = 100 points; 100 × 5.62421 ct × 2.3 = 1293.57 ct → 12,94 EUR.
        assert_eq!(unit_fee_cents(FeeSchedule::Goz, 100, 2.3), 1294);
    }

    #[test]
    fn goz_factor_one_rounding() {
        // 100 × 5.62421 = 562.421 → 5,62 EUR.
        assert_eq!(unit_fee_cents(FeeSchedule::Goz, 100, 1.0), 562);
    }

    #[test]
    fn vat_groups_and_gross() {
        let inv = Invoice {
            lines: vec![
                InvoiceLine {
                    amount_cents: 10_000,
                    vat_percent: Some(19.0),
                    fee_schedule: FeeSchedule::Lab,
                    ..Default::default()
                },
                InvoiceLine {
                    amount_cents: 5_000,
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            ],
            ..Default::default()
        };
        let t = compute_totals(&inv);
        assert_eq!(t.vat_groups, vec![(1900, 10_000, 1_900)]);
        assert_eq!(t.gross, 16_900);
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/src/infrastructure/pdf/core.rs
