//! Clinical document PDFs — certificate, prescription, receipt.
//!
//! These renderers use the shared [`super::core`] / [`super::letterhead`]
//! layout system so all MeDoc PDFs (invoice, record, certificate, prescription,
//! receipt) have **identical margins, fonts, encoding, and page numbers**.
//!
//! Each document type has its own render function that interprets the shared
//! [`ClinicalPdfLayout`] input schema.
//!
//! Input = structured JSON (generated by the frontend).
//! Output = PDF bytes with DIN-5008 letterhead, signature block, and
//! privacy notice at the foot.

use crate::error::AppError;
use serde::Deserialize;

use super::core::{
    emit_multipage_pdf_with_images, emit_pdf_document, pdf_locale, table_row_height, wrap_soft,
    wrap_text, PageBuilder, PdfDocMeta, CONTENT_WIDTH, M_BOTTOM, M_LEFT, M_RIGHT,
};
use super::metrics::wrap_to_width;
use super::letterhead::{
    emit_continuation_header_locale, emit_letterhead, emit_signature_block, stamp_label,
    Letterhead, MetaRow,
};
use super::logo::PdfLogo;

// ===========================================================================
// Input schemas
// ===========================================================================

/// A "label: value" row (e.g. "Vers.-Nr.: A123…").
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct LabelValue {
    pub label: String,
    pub value: String,
    /// Medical content (diagnosis, ICD-10, findings). Removed from copies
    /// that go to third parties (AU employer copy).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub confidential: bool,
    /// Marks the grand-total row (bold, ruled) — replaces label sniffing.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub total: bool,
}

/// Column layout for tables.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TableColumnLayout {
    /// Receipt: date (wide), description (wide), amount (narrow right).
    Receipt,
    /// Prescription (single): med · strength · dose · duration · PZN
    Prescription,
    /// Prescription (combo): pos · med · dose · duration · notes
    PrescriptionCombo,
    /// Even distribution (default).
    #[default]
    Even,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PdfTableSpec {
    pub title: Option<String>,
    pub headers: Vec<String>,
    pub rows: Vec<Vec<String>>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub column_layout: TableColumnLayout,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct DetailRecord {
    pub kind: String,
    pub period: String,
    pub details: Vec<String>,
}

/// Two-column box (e.g. patient data left + insurance right).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct TwoColumnBlock {
    pub left_title: Option<String>,
    pub left_lines: Vec<String>,
    pub right_title: Option<String>,
    pub right_lines: Vec<String>,
}

/// Full input for a clinical document rendering call.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ClinicalPdfLayout {
    /// "certificate" | "prescription" | "receipt" | otherwise default
    pub kind: String,

    /// Practice letterhead (line 1 bold = name).
    pub practice_lines: Vec<String>,

    /// Right column at top — only when `meta_lines` is empty.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub header_right_lines: Vec<String>,

    /// Structured meta block (invoice no., date, …).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub meta_lines: Vec<LabelValue>,

    /// Address field (recipient).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub address_lines: Vec<String>,

    pub document_title: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub document_subtitle: Option<String>,

    /// Introductory paragraphs.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub intro_paragraphs: Vec<String>,

    /// Structured fields ("Label: Value" — compact single-line when possible).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub label_value_rows: Vec<LabelValue>,

    /// Patient panel (patient left, insurance right, etc.).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub two_column: Option<TwoColumnBlock>,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub tables: Vec<PdfTableSpec>,

    /// Detail blocks (e.g. sick-leave period per diagnosis).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub detail_records: Vec<DetailRecord>,

    /// Totals rows (for receipt / invoice).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub totals: Vec<LabelValue>,

    /// Closing paragraphs (e.g. notices, instructions).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub closing_paragraphs: Vec<String>,

    /// Signature block — line 1 = name, then professional title, ZANR, BSNR.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub signature_lines: Vec<String>,

    /// Footer right (issue date, place).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub footer_meta_lines: Vec<LabelValue>,

    /// Provider professional title for the default signature block.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub clinician_professional_title: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub clinician_zanr: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub clinician_bsnr: Option<String>,

    /// Practice logo from Settings (`practice.logo.v1`) — `{mime,data}` base64.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub logo_kv_json: Option<String>,

    /// UI locale (`en`|`de`|`fr`|`ar`) for chrome + page numbers.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub locale: Option<String>,

    /// When true (Arabic), logo is placed top-right.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub rtl: bool,

    /// Which copy is rendered: `patient` (default) | `employer` | `insurer`.
    /// `employer` strips every `confidential` row and all detail records.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub copy_variant: Option<String>,

    /// Registry number printed in the information block (set by the backend).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub document_number: Option<String>,

    /// Diagonal watermark ("ENTWURF" for previews, "KOPIE" for reprints).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub watermark: Option<String>,

    /// DIN footer lines (tax, bank, chamber) — set by the backend.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub footer_lines: Vec<String>,
}

impl ClinicalPdfLayout {
    /// Copy of the layout with confidential rows removed when the copy goes
    /// to a third party (AU-Richtlinie: employer copy without diagnosis).
    pub fn for_copy_variant(&self) -> ClinicalPdfLayout {
        let mut out = self.clone();
        let employer = matches!(out.copy_variant.as_deref(), Some("employer"));
        if employer {
            out.label_value_rows.retain(|r| !r.confidential);
            out.meta_lines.retain(|r| !r.confidential);
            out.footer_meta_lines.retain(|r| !r.confidential);
            out.detail_records.clear();
            out.intro_paragraphs.retain(|p| !p.contains("ICD"));
        }
        out
    }

    fn copy_variant_label(&self, locale: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
        let de = pdf_locale(locale) == "de";
        match self.copy_variant.as_deref() {
            Some("employer") => Some(if de {
                "Ausfertigung zur Vorlage beim Arbeitgeber"
            } else {
                "Copy for the employer"
            }),
            Some("insurer") => Some(if de {
                "Ausfertigung zur Vorlage bei der Krankenkasse"
            } else {
                "Copy for the health insurer"
            }),
            Some("patient") => Some(if de {
                "Ausfertigung für die versicherte Person"
            } else {
                "Copy for the patient"
            }),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

// ===========================================================================
// Column layouts per document type
// ===========================================================================

/// Return X start positions per column for a given layout.
fn table_column_xs(layout: TableColumnLayout, ncol: usize) -> Vec<i32> {
    let ncol = ncol.max(1);
    match layout {
        // Date · Item (receipt no.) · Description — balanced for QU-… numbers
        TableColumnLayout::Receipt if ncol >= 3 => {
            vec![M_LEFT, M_LEFT + 88, M_LEFT + 250]
        }
        TableColumnLayout::Prescription if ncol >= 5 => vec![
            M_LEFT,
            M_LEFT + 130,
            M_LEFT + 220,
            M_LEFT + 295,
            M_LEFT + 365,
        ],
        TableColumnLayout::PrescriptionCombo if ncol >= 5 => vec![
            M_LEFT,
            M_LEFT + 38,
            M_LEFT + 170,
            M_LEFT + 290,
            M_LEFT + 365,
        ],
        _ => {
            let step = CONTENT_WIDTH / ncol as i32;
            (0..ncol).map(|i| M_LEFT + i as i32 * step).collect()
        }
    }
}

/// Usable width (pt) per column from the X positions (minus cell padding).
/// True when every non-empty cell of the column is a money/number value
/// ("120,00 EUR", "-12,94", "1.234,50 €"). Such columns are right-aligned
/// so decimal places line up, as on any accounting document.
fn is_amount_column(table: &PdfTableSpec, ci: usize) -> bool {
    let mut any = false;
    for row in &table.rows {
        let cell = row.get(ci).map(|s| s.trim()).unwrap_or("");
        if cell.is_empty() || cell == "—" || cell == "-" {
            continue;
        }
        let core = cell
            .trim_end_matches("EUR")
            .trim_end_matches('€')
            .trim();
        // Needs a currency or a decimal comma, so dates ("02.11.2026") and
        // fee numbers ("0010") stay left-aligned.
        let money_hint = core.len() != cell.len()
            || core.rsplit_once(',').is_some_and(|(_, d)| d.len() == 2);
        let ok = money_hint
            && core.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())
            && core
                .chars()
                .all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || matches!(c, '.' | ',' | '-' | '+' | ' ' | '\u{a0}'));
        if !ok {
            return false;
        }
        any = true;
    }
    any
}

/// Draw one cell, right-aligned inside the column for amount columns.
fn draw_cell(pb: &mut PageBuilder, x: i32, width: i32, size: i32, bold: bool, s: &str, right: bool) {
    if right {
        pb.text_right(x + TABLE_CELL_PAD + width, size, bold, s);
    } else {
        pb.text(x + TABLE_CELL_PAD, size, bold, s);
    }
}

fn column_pt_widths(xs: &[i32]) -> Vec<i32> {
    let mut w = Vec::with_capacity(xs.len());
    for i in 0..xs.len() {
        let next = xs.get(i + 1).copied().unwrap_or(M_RIGHT);
        w.push((next - xs[i] - 2 * TABLE_CELL_PAD).max(20));
    }
    w
}

// ===========================================================================
// Render function
// ===========================================================================

/// `LabelValue` → `MetaRow` conversion for the letterhead.
fn to_meta_rows(items: &[LabelValue]) -> Vec<MetaRow> {
    items
        .iter()
        .map(|x| MetaRow::new(&x.label, &x.value))
        .collect()
}

/// Main entry: render a clinical PDF.
pub fn render_clinical_layout(doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    render_clinical_layout_with_logo(doc, None)
}

/// Same as [`render_clinical_layout`], with an optional pre-loaded logo
/// (e.g. from `app_kv` when the layout JSON has no `logoKvJson`).
pub fn render_clinical_layout_with_logo(
    doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout,
    logo_override: Option<&PdfLogo>,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let variant = doc.for_copy_variant();
    let doc = &variant;
    let owned_logo = doc.logo_kv_json.as_deref().and_then(PdfLogo::from_kv_json);
    let logo = logo_override.or(owned_logo.as_ref());
    let locale = pdf_locale(doc.locale.as_deref().unwrap_or("de"));

    let mut pb = PageBuilder::new();
    pb.locale = locale;
    pb.watermark = doc.watermark.clone().filter(|w| !w.trim().is_empty());
    pb.footer_lines = doc.footer_lines.clone();
    pb.set_continuation(
        doc.practice_lines.first().map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("MeDoc"),
        &doc.document_title,
        locale,
    );

    match doc.kind.as_str() {
        "certificate" => render_certificate(&mut pb, doc, logo, locale),
        "prescription" => render_prescription(&mut pb, doc, logo, locale),
        "receipt" => render_receipt(&mut pb, doc, logo, locale),
        _ => render_generic(&mut pb, doc, logo, locale),
    }

    emit_clinical_privacy_footer(&mut pb, &doc.kind, locale);

    let pages = pb.finish();
    let images: Vec<PdfLogo> = logo.cloned().into_iter().collect();
    emit_pdf_document(
        &pages,
        &PdfDocMeta {
            title: match doc.document_number.as_deref() {
                Some(n) => format!("{} {n}", doc.document_title),
                None => doc.document_title.clone(),
            },
            subject: doc.document_number.clone(),
            author: doc.practice_lines.first().cloned(),
            locale: locale.to_string(),
        },
        &images,
    )
}

/// Information-block rows incl. the registry number (if issued).
fn meta_rows_with_number(doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout, locale: &str) -> Vec<MetaRow> {
    let mut rows = to_meta_rows(&doc.meta_lines);
    if let Some(n) = doc.document_number.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
        let label = if pdf_locale(locale) == "de" { "Dokument-Nr." } else { "Document no." };
        if !rows.iter().any(|r| r.value == n) {
            rows.insert(0, MetaRow::new(label, n));
        }
    }
    rows
}

/// Prints the copy-variant line ("Ausfertigung zur Vorlage beim Arbeitgeber").
fn emit_copy_variant_line(pb: &mut PageBuilder, doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout, locale: &str) {
    if let Some(label) = doc.copy_variant_label(locale) {
        pb.text_center(9, true, label);
        pb.advance(14);
    }
}

fn clinical_letterhead<'a>(
    doc: &'a ClinicalPdfLayout,
    meta: &'a [MetaRow],
    logo: Option<&'a PdfLogo>,
    locale: &'a str,
) -> Letterhead<'a> {
    Letterhead {
        practice_lines: &doc.practice_lines,
        meta_rows: meta,
        address_lines: &doc.address_lines,
        header_right_lines: &doc.header_right_lines,
        show_sender_hint: !doc.address_lines.is_empty(),
        compact_address: doc.kind == "receipt",
        logo,
        rtl: doc.rtl,
        locale,
    }
}

/// Plain preview when the frontend sends `bodyLines` only (no `layoutJson`).
/// Uses the same letterhead + footer stack as structured clinical exports.
pub fn render_plain_preview(
    kind: &str,
    template_name: &str,
    footer: &str,
    body_pt: i32,
    body_lines: &[String],
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let fs = body_pt.clamp(8, 18);
    let wrap_cols = 86usize.saturating_sub((18 - fs) as usize * 2);

    let cleaned: Vec<String> = body_lines
        .iter()
        .map(|l| crate::infrastructure::clinical_text_format::plain_text_for_pdf(l))
        .collect();

    let practice_lines: Vec<String> = cleaned
        .first()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
        .map(|s| vec![s.clone()])
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let body_start = if practice_lines.is_empty() { 0 } else { 1 };

    let mut pb = PageBuilder::new();
    let lh = Letterhead {
        practice_lines: &practice_lines,
        meta_rows: &[],
        address_lines: &[],
        header_right_lines: &[],
        show_sender_hint: false,
        compact_address: false,
        logo: None,
        rtl: false,
        locale: "de",
    };
    emit_letterhead(&mut pb, &lh);

    pb.text(M_LEFT, 14, true, template_name);
    pb.advance(18);
    pb.text(M_LEFT, 9, false, &format!("Dokumentart: {kind}"));
    pb.advance(14);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(16);

    for raw in &cleaned[body_start..] {
        if raw.trim().is_empty() {
            pb.advance(fs / 2);
            continue;
        }
        pb.paragraph(raw, fs, wrap_cols, 0);
    }

    if !footer.trim().is_empty() {
        pb.advance(8);
        pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
        pb.advance(10);
        for chunk in wrap_soft(footer.trim(), wrap_cols) {
            pb.text(M_LEFT, 8, false, &chunk);
            pb.advance(11);
        }
    }

    emit_clinical_privacy_footer(&mut pb, kind, "de");

    // This path renders free text without a registry entry → never official.
    pb.watermark = Some("ENTWURF".into());
    let pages = pb.finish();
    emit_multipage_pdf_with_images(&pages, &format!("Vorschau {template_name}"), &[], "de")
}

fn emit_clinical_privacy_footer(pb: &mut PageBuilder, _kind: &str, locale: &str) {
    // Receipts name the patient and the treatment, so they carry the notice too.
    let notice = match pdf_locale(locale) {
        "de" => "Vertrauliche Patientendaten gem. § 630f BGB / Art. 9 DSGVO. Weitergabe nur an berechtigte Personen.",
        "fr" => "Données patient confidentielles (art. 9 RGPD). Divulgation uniquement aux personnes autorisées.",
        _ => "Confidential patient data pursuant to § 630f BGB / Art. 9 GDPR. Disclosure only to authorized parties.",
    };
    pb.ensure_space(40);
    pb.advance(12);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(10);
    pb.text(M_LEFT, 7, false, notice);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// CERTIFICATE  (Form-1 style)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn render_certificate(
    pb: &mut PageBuilder,
    doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout,
    logo: Option<&PdfLogo>,
    locale: &str,
) {
    // Letterhead — certificates usually go to the patient directly, so
    // address_lines apply
    let meta_rows = meta_rows_with_number(doc, locale);
    let lh = clinical_letterhead(doc, &meta_rows, logo, locale);
    emit_letterhead(pb, &lh);

    // Centered title
    pb.text_center(17, true, &doc.document_title);
    pb.advance(22);
    if let Some(sub) = doc
        .document_subtitle
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        pb.text_center(10, false, sub);
        pb.advance(14);
    }

    emit_copy_variant_line(pb, doc, locale);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT + 100, M_RIGHT - 100);
    pb.advance(16);

    if let Some(ref tc) = doc.two_column {
        emit_two_column_panel(pb, tc);
    }
    render_intro(pb, &doc.intro_paragraphs);
    emit_label_value_rows(pb, &doc.label_value_rows);

    for table in &doc.tables {
        emit_table(
            pb,
            table,
            (
                doc.practice_lines
                    .first()
                    .map(|s| s.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or("MeDoc"),
                &doc.document_title,
            ),
        );
    }

    emit_detail_records(pb, &doc.detail_records);

    for p in &doc.closing_paragraphs {
        if !p.trim().is_empty() {
            pb.paragraph(p, 10, 84, 0);
        }
    }

    emit_default_or_custom_signature(pb, doc);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// PRESCRIPTION  (Form-16 style — private prescription)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn render_prescription(
    pb: &mut PageBuilder,
    doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout,
    logo: Option<&PdfLogo>,
    locale: &str,
) {
    let meta_rows = meta_rows_with_number(doc, locale);
    let lh = clinical_letterhead(doc, &meta_rows, logo, locale);
    emit_letterhead(pb, &lh);

    // Large "Prescription" title on the left, optional subtitle (private / statutory / BtM)
    pb.text(M_LEFT, 18, true, &doc.document_title);
    pb.advance(22);
    if let Some(sub) = doc
        .document_subtitle
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        pb.text(M_LEFT, 10, false, sub);
        pb.advance(14);
    }
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(18);

    // Patient panel (two columns)
    if let Some(ref tc) = doc.two_column {
        emit_two_column_panel(pb, tc);
    }

    // "Rp." (the prescribed drugs) comes first, then duration / aut idem / notes.
    if !doc.tables.is_empty() {
        pb.advance(6);
        for table in &doc.tables {
            emit_table(
                pb,
                table,
                (
                    doc.practice_lines
                        .first()
                        .map(|s| s.as_str())
                        .unwrap_or("MeDoc"),
                    &doc.document_title,
                ),
            );
        }
        pb.advance(6);
    }

    emit_label_value_rows(pb, &doc.label_value_rows);

    render_intro(pb, &doc.intro_paragraphs);

    for p in &doc.closing_paragraphs {
        if !p.trim().is_empty() {
            pb.paragraph(p, 10, 84, 0);
        }
    }

    emit_default_or_custom_signature(pb, doc);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// RECEIPT  (GoBD-compliant)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn render_receipt(
    pb: &mut PageBuilder,
    doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout,
    logo: Option<&PdfLogo>,
    locale: &str,
) {
    let meta_rows = meta_rows_with_number(doc, locale);
    let lh = clinical_letterhead(doc, &meta_rows, logo, locale);
    emit_letterhead(pb, &lh);

    pb.text_center(15, true, &doc.document_title);
    pb.advance(18);
    if let Some(sub) = doc
        .document_subtitle
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        pb.text_center(11, false, sub);
        pb.advance(14);
    }
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(14);

    render_intro(pb, &doc.intro_paragraphs);
    emit_label_value_rows(pb, &doc.label_value_rows);

    let cont = (
        doc.practice_lines
            .first()
            .map(|s| s.as_str())
            .unwrap_or("MeDoc"),
        doc.document_title.as_str(),
    );
    // Service lines first, then money totals (standard receipt order).
    for table in &doc.tables {
        emit_table(pb, table, cont);
    }
    emit_receipt_totals(pb, &doc.totals);

    pb.advance(6);
    for p in &doc.closing_paragraphs {
        if !p.trim().is_empty() {
            pb.paragraph(p, 9, 90, 0);
        }
    }

    emit_default_or_custom_signature(pb, doc);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GENERIC  (fallback for unknown kind)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn render_generic(
    pb: &mut PageBuilder,
    doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout,
    logo: Option<&PdfLogo>,
    locale: &str,
) {
    let meta_rows = meta_rows_with_number(doc, locale);
    let lh = clinical_letterhead(doc, &meta_rows, logo, locale);
    emit_letterhead(pb, &lh);

    pb.text(M_LEFT, 14, true, &doc.document_title);
    pb.advance(18);
    if let Some(sub) = doc
        .document_subtitle
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
    {
        pb.text(M_LEFT, 10, false, sub);
        pb.advance(14);
    }

    render_intro(pb, &doc.intro_paragraphs);
    emit_label_value_rows(pb, &doc.label_value_rows);

    if let Some(ref tc) = doc.two_column {
        emit_two_column_panel(pb, tc);
    }

    for table in &doc.tables {
        emit_table(
            pb,
            table,
            (
                doc.practice_lines
                    .first()
                    .map(|s| s.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or("MeDoc"),
                &doc.document_title,
            ),
        );
    }

    emit_detail_records(pb, &doc.detail_records);
    emit_totals_block(pb, &doc.totals);

    for p in &doc.closing_paragraphs {
        if !p.trim().is_empty() {
            pb.paragraph(p, 10, 84, 0);
        }
    }

    emit_default_or_custom_signature(pb, doc);
}

// ===========================================================================
// Bausteine
// ===========================================================================

fn render_intro(pb: &mut PageBuilder, paragraphs: &[String]) {
    for p in paragraphs {
        if p.trim().is_empty() {
            pb.advance(6);
            continue;
        }
        // Heuristic: if the element is a colon headline
        // ("Notee:", "Diagnosis:") AND short AND no further colons
        // inside, treat it as a section title.
        let trimmed = p.trim_end();
        let is_heading = trimmed.ends_with(':')
            && trimmed.len() <= 40
            && trimmed[..trimmed.len() - 1]
                .chars()
                .filter(|c| *c == ':')
                .count()
                == 0;

        if is_heading {
            pb.ensure_space(40);
            pb.advance(6);
            pb.text(M_LEFT, 11, true, &trimmed[..trimmed.len() - 1]);
            pb.advance(14);
        } else {
            pb.paragraph(p, 10, 84, 0);
            pb.advance(3);
        }
    }
}

fn emit_label_value_rows(pb: &mut PageBuilder, rows: &[LabelValue]) {
    // Value column starts after the widest label (measured), so long German
    // labels like "Voraussichtlich bis einschließlich" never overlap the value.
    let label_col = rows
        .iter()
        .map(|r| crate::infrastructure::pdf::text_width(&format!("{}:", r.label), 9, true))
        .max()
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .clamp(130, 230)
        + 12;
    let value_pt = f64::from(CONTENT_WIDTH - label_col);
    for row in rows {
        pb.ensure_space(36);
        let multiline = row.value.contains('\n');
        let compact = !multiline
            && crate::infrastructure::pdf::text_width(row.value.trim(), 10, false) as f64 <= value_pt;

        if compact {
            pb.text(M_LEFT, 9, true, &format!("{}:", row.label));
            pb.text(M_LEFT + label_col, 10, false, row.value.trim());
            pb.advance(14);
        } else {
            pb.text(M_LEFT, 9, true, &row.label);
            pb.advance(12);
            // Multi-line values: wrap each line separately so structure
            // is preserved
            for raw in row.value.split('\n') {
                if raw.trim().is_empty() {
                    pb.advance(6);
                    continue;
                }
                for chunk in wrap_text(raw, 84) {
                    pb.ensure_space(30);
                    pb.text(M_LEFT + 4, 10, false, &chunk);
                    pb.advance(12);
                }
            }
            pb.advance(6);
        }
    }
}

fn emit_two_column_panel(pb: &mut PageBuilder, block: &TwoColumnBlock) {
    let col_pt = f64::from(CONTENT_WIDTH / 2 - 16);
    let count = |lines: &[String]| -> i32 {
        lines
            .iter()
            .map(|l| wrap_to_width(l, 9, false, col_pt).len() as i32)
            .sum()
    };
    let rows = count(&block.left_lines).max(count(&block.right_lines)).max(1);
    // Title band + rows + padding — the box grows with its content.
    let panel_h = 30 + rows * 11;
    pb.ensure_space(panel_h + 30);
    let panel_bottom = pb.y - panel_h + 4;
    let title_band_y = pb.y - 12;

    // Rahmen
    pb.stroke_rect(M_LEFT, panel_bottom, CONTENT_WIDTH, panel_h);
    // Heller Titel-Streifen oben
    pb.fill_rect(M_LEFT + 1, title_band_y, CONTENT_WIDTH - 2, 13, 0.94);

    let mid_x = M_LEFT + CONTENT_WIDTH / 2;
    let title_y = pb.y - 8;

    // Titel
    let prev_y = pb.y;
    pb.y = title_y;
    pb.text(
        M_LEFT + 8,
        9,
        true,
        block.left_title.as_deref().unwrap_or("Patient"),
    );
    if let Some(t) = block.right_title.as_deref() {
        pb.text(mid_x + 4, 9, true, t);
    }
    pb.y = prev_y;

    // Linke Spalte
    let mut yl = title_y - 14;
    for line in &block.left_lines {
        for chunk in wrap_to_width(line, 9, false, col_pt) {
            pb.y = yl;
            pb.text(M_LEFT + 10, 9, false, &chunk);
            yl -= 11;
        }
    }

    // Rechte Spalte
    let mut yr = title_y - 14;
    for line in &block.right_lines {
        for chunk in wrap_to_width(line, 9, false, col_pt) {
            pb.y = yr;
            pb.text(mid_x + 6, 9, false, &chunk);
            yr -= 11;
        }
    }

    pb.y = panel_bottom - 18;
}

fn emit_table(pb: &mut PageBuilder, table: &PdfTableSpec, continuation: (&str, &str)) {
    let ncol = table
        .headers
        .len()
        .max(table.rows.iter().map(|r| r.len()).max().unwrap_or(0))
        .max(1);
    let col_xs = table_column_xs(table.column_layout, ncol);
    let col_widths = column_pt_widths(&col_xs);

    if let Some(t) = table.title.as_deref().filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {
        pb.ensure_space(80);
        pb.advance(4);
        pb.text(M_LEFT, 11, true, t);
        pb.advance(14);
    }

    pb.ensure_space(70);

    // Table header — same inset as body cells
    pb.table_header_band(M_LEFT, pb.y + 2, CONTENT_WIDTH);
    for (ci, h) in table.headers.iter().enumerate() {
        let x = col_xs.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(M_LEFT);
        let w = col_widths.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(60);
        draw_cell(pb, x, w, 9, true, h, is_amount_column(table, ci));
    }
    pb.advance(14);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(10);

    emit_table_data_rows(pb, table, &col_xs, &col_widths, continuation);
}

fn emit_detail_records(pb: &mut PageBuilder, records: &[DetailRecord]) {
    for rec in records {
        let detail_lines: usize = rec
            .details
            .iter()
            .map(|d| wrap_soft(d, 80).len().max(1))
            .sum();
        let total_h = 14 + 24 + 14 + (detail_lines as i32 * 11) + 12;
        pb.ensure_space(total_h);

        let box_bottom = pb.y - total_h + 8;
        pb.fill_rect(M_LEFT, box_bottom, CONTENT_WIDTH, total_h - 8, 0.97);

        pb.advance(2);
        let de = pb.locale == "de";
        pb.text(M_LEFT + 6, 9, true, if de { "Art" } else { "Type" });
        pb.advance(12);
        for chunk in wrap_soft(&rec.kind, 80) {
            pb.text(M_LEFT + 6, 9, false, &chunk);
            pb.advance(11);
        }
        pb.advance(2);

        pb.text(M_LEFT + 6, 9, true, if de { "Zeitraum" } else { "Period" });
        pb.advance(12);
        for chunk in wrap_soft(&rec.period, 80) {
            pb.text(M_LEFT + 6, 9, false, &chunk);
            pb.advance(11);
        }
        pb.advance(2);

        pb.text(M_LEFT + 6, 9, true, if de { "Angaben" } else { "Details" });
        pb.advance(12);
        for detail in &rec.details {
            for chunk in wrap_soft(detail, 80) {
                pb.text(M_LEFT + 6, 9, false, &chunk);
                pb.advance(11);
            }
        }
        pb.advance(8);
    }
}

/// Uniform cell inset for table headers and body (must match).
const TABLE_CELL_PAD: i32 = 6;
/// Uniform padding in receipt totals.
const RECEIPT_PAD: i32 = 10;
const RECEIPT_ROW_H: i32 = 16;

/// Grand-total row: explicit `total` flag; label sniffing only as fallback
/// for layouts that do not set the flag yet.
fn is_total_row(row: &LabelValue, all: &[LabelValue]) -> bool {
    if all.iter().any(|r| r.total) {
        return row.total;
    }
    let l = row.label.to_lowercase();
    l == "total" || l == "gesamt" || l.starts_with("gesamtbetrag") || l.starts_with("endbetrag")
}

fn emit_receipt_totals(pb: &mut PageBuilder, totals: &[LabelValue]) {
    if totals.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    pb.ensure_space(24 + totals.len() as i32 * RECEIPT_ROW_H);
    pb.advance(10);
    // Separator above the money block (keeps the Total row free of overlapping rules).
    pb.hline(M_LEFT + 220, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(12);

    for row in totals {
        let bold = is_total_row(row, totals);
        if bold {
            pb.advance(2);
            pb.hline(M_LEFT + 220, M_RIGHT);
            pb.advance(12);
        }
        pb.text(M_LEFT + 220, 10, bold, &format!("{}:", row.label));
        let value = super::core::sanitize_pdf_money(&row.value);
        pb.text_right(M_RIGHT - RECEIPT_PAD, 10, bold, &value);
        pb.advance(RECEIPT_ROW_H);
    }
    pb.advance(4);
}

/// Table rows without re-emitting the header band (after page break).
fn emit_table_data_rows(
    pb: &mut PageBuilder,
    table: &PdfTableSpec,
    col_xs: &[i32],
    col_widths: &[i32],
    continuation: (&str, &str),
) {
    let ncol = table
        .headers
        .len()
        .max(table.rows.iter().map(|r| r.len()).max().unwrap_or(0))
        .max(1);

    let amount_cols: Vec<bool> = (0..ncol).map(|ci| is_amount_column(table, ci)).collect();
    for (ri, row) in table.rows.iter().enumerate() {
        let mut wrapped: Vec<Vec<String>> = Vec::with_capacity(ncol);
        let mut row_lines = 1usize;
        for ci in 0..ncol {
            let cell = row.get(ci).map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
            let max_pt = col_widths.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(60);
            // Wrap by measured width; never truncate clinical content.
            let w = wrap_to_width(cell, 9, false, f64::from(max_pt));
            row_lines = row_lines.max(w.len().max(1));
            wrapped.push(w);
        }

        let font_size = 9;
        let line_step = 12;
        let row_height = table_row_height(row_lines, line_step, font_size);
        if pb.y < M_BOTTOM + row_height + 20 {
            pb.break_page();
            let loc = pb.locale;
            emit_continuation_header_locale(pb, continuation.0, continuation.1, loc);
            pb.table_header_band(M_LEFT, pb.y + 2, CONTENT_WIDTH);
            for (ci, h) in table.headers.iter().enumerate() {
                let x = col_xs.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(M_LEFT);
                let w = col_widths.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(60);
                draw_cell(pb, x, w, 9, true, h, is_amount_column(table, ci));
            }
            pb.advance(14);
            pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
            pb.advance(10);
        }

        let first_baseline = pb.y;
        if ri % 2 == 1 {
            pb.fill_table_row_band(
                M_LEFT,
                CONTENT_WIDTH,
                first_baseline,
                row_height,
                font_size,
                0.97,
            );
        }

        for li in 0..row_lines {
            for ci in 0..ncol {
                let x = col_xs.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(M_LEFT);
                let chunk = wrapped
                    .get(ci)
                    .and_then(|w| w.get(li))
                    .map(|s| s.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or("");
                if !chunk.is_empty() {
                    let prev = pb.y;
                    pb.y = first_baseline - li as i32 * line_step;
                    let w = col_widths.get(ci).copied().unwrap_or(60);
                    draw_cell(pb, x, w, font_size, false, chunk, amount_cols[ci]);
                    pb.y = prev;
                }
            }
        }
        pb.advance(row_height);
    }
    pb.advance(8);
}

fn emit_totals_block(pb: &mut PageBuilder, totals: &[LabelValue]) {
    if totals.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    pb.ensure_space(40 + totals.len() as i32 * 14);
    pb.advance(8);
    pb.hline(300, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(3);
    pb.hline(300, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(14);
    for row in totals {
        let bold = is_total_row(row, totals);
        pb.text_right(380, 10, bold, &format!("{}:", row.label));
        pb.text_right(
            M_RIGHT,
            10,
            bold,
            &super::core::sanitize_pdf_money(&row.value),
        );
        pb.advance(14);
    }
    pb.advance(8);
}

/// When `signature_lines` is populated, use those lines directly;
/// otherwise use the default signature block from `behandler_*` fields.
fn emit_default_or_custom_signature(pb: &mut PageBuilder, doc: &ClinicalPdfLayout) {
    if doc.signature_lines.is_empty() {
        // No custom block — default signature block. Provider name
        // is not available separately on this path; only professional
        // title, ZANR, and BSNR are emitted.
        emit_signature_block(
            pb,
            None,
            doc.clinician_professional_title.as_deref(),
            doc.clinician_zanr.as_deref(),
            doc.clinician_bsnr.as_deref(),
            true,
        );
        emit_footer_meta(pb, &doc.footer_meta_lines);
        return;
    }

    // Custom signature lines
    pb.ensure_space(80);
    pb.advance(20);
    pb.hline(M_LEFT, M_RIGHT);
    pb.advance(20);
    pb.hline_at(M_LEFT, pb.y, M_LEFT + 180);
    pb.advance(14);
    for (i, line) in doc.signature_lines.iter().enumerate() {
        let bold = i == 0;
        let size = if i == 0 { 10 } else { 9 };
        pb.text(M_LEFT, size, bold, line);
        pb.advance(size + 2);
    }
    pb.advance(4);
    let stamp = stamp_label(pb.locale);
    pb.text(M_LEFT, 8, false, stamp);

    emit_footer_meta(pb, &doc.footer_meta_lines);
}

fn emit_footer_meta(pb: &mut PageBuilder, meta: &[LabelValue]) {
    if meta.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    // Ensure meta lines appear below the signature block
    pb.advance(10);
    for row in meta {
        pb.text(M_LEFT, 8, false, &row.label);
        pb.text_right(M_RIGHT, 9, false, &row.value);
        pb.advance(12);
    }
}

    #[test]
    fn explicit_total_flag_wins_over_label() {
        let rows = vec![
            LabelValue { label: "Subtotal".into(), value: "1".into(), ..Default::default() },
            LabelValue { label: "Zu zahlen".into(), value: "1".into(), total: true, ..Default::default() },
        ];
        assert!(!is_total_row(&rows[0], &rows));
        assert!(is_total_row(&rows[1], &rows));
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/src/infrastructure/pdf/metrics.rs

//! Glyph widths for the two base-14 fonts MeDoc uses (Helvetica, Helvetica-Bold).
//!
//! Values are the advance widths from Adobe's Core14 AFM files (units of
//! 1/1000 em). Replaces the old `chars × 0.52 × size` guess, which made
//! right-aligned amounts and column wrapping drift.
//!
//! When the renderer moves to embedded fonts (PDF/A, Arabic), this module is
//! replaced by the font's own `hmtx` table.

/// Advance width of `ch` in 1/1000 em.
pub fn glyph_width(ch: char, bold: bool) -> u32 {
    let c = ch as u32;
    if (32..=126).contains(&c) {
        let idx = (c - 32) as usize;
        return if bold {
            BOLD_ASCII[idx]
        } else {
            REGULAR_ASCII[idx]
        } as u32;
    }
    // Accented letters share the width of their base letter in Helvetica.
    let base = match ch {
        'ä' | 'à' | 'á' | 'â' | 'å' => Some('a'),
        'Ä' | 'À' | 'Á' | 'Â' | 'Å' => Some('A'),
        'ö' | 'ò' | 'ó' | 'ô' | 'ø' => Some('o'),
        'Ö' | 'Ò' | 'Ó' | 'Ô' | 'Ø' => Some('O'),
        'ü' | 'ù' | 'ú' | 'û' => Some('u'),
        'Ü' | 'Ù' | 'Ú' | 'Û' => Some('U'),
        'é' | 'è' | 'ê' | 'ë' => Some('e'),
        'É' | 'È' | 'Ê' | 'Ë' => Some('E'),
        'í' | 'ì' | 'î' | 'ï' => Some('i'),
        'ç' => Some('c'),
        'Ç' => Some('C'),
        'ñ' => Some('n'),
        'Ñ' => Some('N'),
        'š' => Some('s'),
        'Š' => Some('S'),
        'ž' => Some('z'),
        'Ž' => Some('Z'),
        _ => None,
    };
    if let Some(b) = base {
        return glyph_width(b, bold);
    }
    match ch {
        'ß' => 611,
        '€' => 556,
        '§' => 556,
        '°' => 400,
        '×' | '÷' | '±' => 584,
        '–' => 556,
        '—' | '…' | '‰' => 1000,
        '•' => 350,
        '\u{00A0}' => 278,
        '„' | '“' | '”' => {
            if bold {
                500
            } else {
                333
            }
        }
        '‚' | '‘' | '’' => {
            if bold {
                278
            } else {
                222
            }
        }
        '²' | '³' | '¹' => 333,
        '½' | '¼' | '¾' => 834,
        _ => 556,
    }
}

/// Width of `s` in PDF points at `font_size`.
pub fn text_width_pt(s: &str, font_size: i32, bold: bool) -> f64 {
    let units: u32 = s.chars().map(|c| glyph_width(c, bold)).sum();
    f64::from(units) * f64::from(font_size) / 1000.0
}

/// Largest prefix (in chars) of `s` that fits into `max_pt`.
pub fn fit_chars(s: &str, font_size: i32, bold: bool, max_pt: f64) -> usize {
    let mut acc = 0.0;
    for (i, c) in s.chars().enumerate() {
        acc += f64::from(glyph_width(c, bold)) * f64::from(font_size) / 1000.0;
        if acc > max_pt {
            return i;
        }
    }
    s.chars().count()
}

/// Wrap on whitespace so that every line fits into `max_pt`. Words longer
/// than the line are hard-broken (never silently clipped).
pub fn wrap_to_width(text: &str, font_size: i32, bold: bool, max_pt: f64) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for paragraph in text.split('\n') {
        let trimmed = paragraph.trim_end();
        if trimmed.is_empty() {
            out.push(String::new());
            continue;
        }
        let mut cur = String::new();
        for word in trimmed.split_whitespace() {
            let candidate = if cur.is_empty() {
                word.to_string()
            } else {
                format!("{cur} {word}")
            };
            if text_width_pt(&candidate, font_size, bold) <= max_pt {
                cur = candidate;
                continue;
            }
            if !cur.is_empty() {
                out.push(std::mem::take(&mut cur));
            }
            // Word alone too long → hard break.
            let mut rest: String = word.to_string();
            while text_width_pt(&rest, font_size, bold) > max_pt {
                let n = fit_chars(&rest, font_size, bold, max_pt).max(1);
                let head: String = rest.chars().take(n).collect();
                out.push(head);
                rest = rest.chars().skip(n).collect();
            }
            cur = rest;
        }
        if !cur.is_empty() {
            out.push(cur);
        }
    }
    if out.is_empty() {
        out.push(String::new());
    }
    out
}

#[rustfmt::skip]
const REGULAR_ASCII: [u16; 95] = [
    278, 278, 355, 556, 556, 889, 667, 191, 333, 333, 389, 584, 278, 333, 278, 278, // ' '..'/'
    556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556,                               // 0..9
    278, 278, 584, 584, 584, 556, 1015,                                             // :..@
    667, 667, 722, 722, 667, 611, 778, 722, 278, 500, 667, 556, 833,                // A..M
    722, 778, 667, 778, 722, 667, 611, 722, 667, 944, 667, 667, 611,                // N..Z
    278, 278, 278, 469, 556, 333,                                                   // [..`
    556, 556, 500, 556, 556, 278, 556, 556, 222, 222, 500, 222, 833,                // a..m
    556, 556, 556, 556, 333, 500, 278, 556, 500, 722, 500, 500, 500,                // n..z
    334, 260, 334, 584,                                                             // {..~
];

#[rustfmt::skip]
const BOLD_ASCII: [u16; 95] = [
    278, 333, 474, 556, 556, 889, 722, 238, 333, 333, 389, 584, 278, 333, 278, 278,
    556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556, 556,
    333, 333, 584, 584, 584, 611, 975,
    722, 722, 722, 722, 667, 611, 778, 722, 278, 556, 722, 611, 833,
    722, 778, 667, 778, 722, 667, 611, 722, 667, 944, 667, 667, 611,
    333, 278, 333, 584, 556, 333,
    556, 611, 556, 611, 556, 333, 611, 611, 278, 278, 556, 278, 889,
    611, 611, 611, 611, 389, 556, 333, 611, 556, 778, 556, 556, 500,
    389, 280, 389, 584,
];

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn digits_are_tabular() {
        assert_eq!(
            text_width_pt("1111", 10, false),
            text_width_pt("8888", 10, false)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn bold_is_wider_for_lowercase() {
        assert!(text_width_pt("abc", 10, true) > text_width_pt("abc", 10, false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn umlaut_matches_base() {
        assert_eq!(glyph_width('ä', false), glyph_width('a', false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrap_never_exceeds_width() {
        let lines = wrap_to_width(
            "Zahnfleischbluten Parodontalbehandlungsplanung extrem langes Wort",
            9,
            false,
            60.0,
        );
        assert!(lines.iter().all(|l| text_width_pt(l, 9, false) <= 60.0));
        assert!(lines.len() > 2);
    }
}

//! Renders one sample of every official document through the real issuing
//! path (in-memory database, server-side practice data, numbering, registry)
//! and checks the properties an official German practice document needs.
//!
//! The PDFs are written to `$MEDOC_PDF_SAMPLE_DIR` (default
//! `target/medoc-pdf-samples`) so they can be checked with qpdf/pdftotext and
//! looked at by a person before release.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use base64::Engine;
use medoc_core::application::auth_service::Session;
use medoc_core::application::documents::issue::{
    cancel_invoice, issue_clinical_document, issue_invoice, issue_report, IssueClinicalArgs,
    IssueInvoiceArgs, IssuedPdf,
};
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::app_kv_repo;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use medoc_core::infrastructure::pdf::{
    unit_fee_cents, FeeSchedule, Invoice, InvoiceLine, ReportPdfInput, ReportPdfSection,
    ReportPdfSummaryRow,
};

const PRACTICE: &str = r#"{
  "name": "Zahnarztpraxis Dr. Anna Berger",
  "addr": "Hauptstraße 12\n49074 Osnabrück",
  "phone": "0541 123456", "email": "praxis@berger-zahn.de", "web": "https://berger-zahn.de",
  "clinician_name": "Dr. med. dent. Anna Berger", "professional_title": "Zahnärztin",
  "tax_number": "66/123/45678", "bsnr": "123456789", "zanr": "987654321",
  "bank_name": "Sparkasse Osnabrück", "bank_iban": "DE89 3704 0044 0532 0130 00",
  "bank_bic": "COBADEFFXXX", "account_holder": "Dr. Anna Berger",
  "chamber": "Zahnärztekammer Niedersachsen", "payment_terms_days": 30
}"#;

fn session() -> Session {
    Session {
        user_id: "seed-physician-001".into(),
        name: "Dr. Anna Berger".into(),
        email: "praxis@berger-zahn.de".into(),
        role: "PHYSICIAN".into(),
        permission_overrides: vec![],
        device_session_id: None,
        token_version: 0,
    }
}

fn out_dir() -> PathBuf {
    let dir = std::env::var("MEDOC_PDF_SAMPLE_DIR")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| {
            PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../../target/medoc-pdf-samples")
        });
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).expect("sample dir");
    dir
}

async fn setup() -> sqlx::SqlitePool {
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrations");
    app_kv_repo::set(&pool, "invoice.practice.v1", PRACTICE)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    app_kv_repo::set(&pool, "documents.locale.v1", "\"de\"")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    for sql in [
        "INSERT INTO patient (id, name, date_of_birth, sex, insurance_number, status, address)
         VALUES ('p1', 'Lena Müller', '1990-01-15', 'FEMALE', 'A123456789', 'ACTIVE',
                 'Musterweg 3, 49076 Osnabrück')",
        "INSERT INTO payment (id, patient_id, amount, payment_method, status)
         VALUES ('pay1', 'p1', 120.0, 'CARD', 'PAID')",
        "INSERT INTO certificate (id, patient_id, physician_id, kind, body_text, valid_from, valid_until)
         VALUES ('c1', 'p1', 'seed-physician-001', 'SICK_LEAVE', 'Akute Pulpitis 36', '2026-11-09', '2026-11-11')",
        "INSERT INTO prescription (id, patient_id, physician_id, medication, dosage, duration,
                                   dosage_form, pack_size, quantity, prescription_type, issued_at)
         VALUES ('rx1', 'p1', 'seed-physician-001', 'Ibuprofen 600 mg', '1-0-1 nach dem Essen',
                 '5 Tage', 'Filmtabletten', 'N1 (20 St.)', 1, 'PRIVAT', '2026-11-09')",
    ] {
        sqlx::query(sql).execute(&pool).await.unwrap();
    }
    pool
}

fn bytes(p: &IssuedPdf) -> Vec<u8> {
    base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD
        .decode(&p.pdf_base64)
        .expect("base64")
}

/// WinAnsi bytes as Latin-1 text, with PDF string escapes resolved
/// (`\\ddd` octal, `\\(`, `\\)`, `\\\\`). Content streams are uncompressed.
fn text(pdf: &[u8]) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(pdf.len());
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < pdf.len() {
        let b = pdf[i];
        if b == b'\\' && i + 1 < pdf.len() {
            let n = pdf[i + 1];
            if n.is_ascii_digit() && i + 3 < pdf.len() && pdf[i + 2].is_ascii_digit() && pdf[i + 3].is_ascii_digit() {
                let v = (n - b'0') as u32 * 64 + (pdf[i + 2] - b'0') as u32 * 8 + (pdf[i + 3] - b'0') as u32;
                out.push(char::from_u32(v).unwrap_or('?'));
                i += 4;
                continue;
            }
            if matches!(n, b'(' | b')' | b'\\') {
                out.push(n as char);
                i += 2;
                continue;
            }
        }
        out.push(b as char);
        i += 1;
    }
    out
}

/// Text as it appears in the content stream (WinAnsi = Latin-1 for these chars).
fn latin1(s: &str) -> String {
    s.to_string()
}

/// Checks every official PDF must pass.
fn assert_official(name: &str, pdf: &[u8], number: Option<&str>) -> String {
    let path = out_dir().join(format!("{name}.pdf"));
    std::fs::write(&path, pdf).unwrap();
    let t = text(pdf);
    assert!(t.starts_with("%PDF-1."), "{name}: header");
    assert!(t.trim_end().ends_with("%%EOF"), "{name}: trailer");
    assert!(t.contains("/MediaBox [0 0 595"), "{name}: A4 page size");
    assert!(
        t.contains("/Lang (de-DE)") || t.contains("/Lang (de)"),
        "{name}: /Lang"
    );
    assert!(t.contains("/CreationDate (D:"), "{name}: CreationDate");
    assert!(t.contains("/Title ("), "{name}: Title");
    for needle in ["Zahnarztpraxis Dr. Anna Berger", "Seite 1 von"] {
        assert!(t.contains(&latin1(needle)), "{name}: missing {needle:?}");
    }
    // Legal identity on every official document (footer).
    for needle in ["66/123/45678", "DE89 3704 0044 0532 0130 00"] {
        assert!(t.contains(needle), "{name}: footer missing {needle:?}");
    }
    if let Some(n) = number {
        assert!(t.contains(n), "{name}: document number {n} not printed");
    }
    assert!(
        !t.contains("ENTWURF"),
        "{name}: final document must not carry ENTWURF"
    );
    t
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn render_official_document_samples() {
    let pool = setup().await;
    let s = session();
    let year = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y").to_string();

    // ---- Invoice (GOZ Anlage 2) --------------------------------------------
    let line = |nr: &str, desc: &str, points: u32, factor: f64, tooth: Option<&str>| InvoiceLine {
        description: desc.into(),
        goz_nr: Some(nr.into()),
        points: Some(points),
        factor: Some(factor),
        quantity: Some(1),
        amount_cents: unit_fee_cents(FeeSchedule::Goz, points, factor),
        tooth_nr: tooth.map(String::from),
        treatment_date: Some("2026-11-02".into()),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let mut endo = line(
        "2410",
        "Aufbereitung eines Wurzelkanals",
        392,
        3.0,
        Some("36"),
    );
    endo.diagnosis_reason =
        Some("Stark gekrümmte, obliterierte Kanäle, erhöhter Zeitaufwand".into());
    let invoice = Invoice {
        recipient_name: "Lena Müller".into(),
        recipient_address: vec!["Musterweg 3".into(), "49076 Osnabrück".into()],
        lines: vec![
            line("0010", "Eingehende Untersuchung", 100, 2.3, None),
            line(
                "0090",
                "Intraorale Infiltrationsanästhesie",
                60,
                2.3,
                Some("36"),
            ),
            endo,
            InvoiceLine {
                description: "Wurzelfüllmaterial (Guttapercha, Sealer)".into(),
                fee_schedule: FeeSchedule::Material,
                amount_cents: 1850,
                quantity: Some(1),
                treatment_date: Some("2026-11-02".into()),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        ],
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let inv = issue_invoice(
        &pool,
        &s,
        IssueInvoiceArgs {
            patient_id: "p1".into(),
            invoice,
            preview: false,
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("issue invoice");
    let inv_no = format!("RE-{year}-000001");
    assert_eq!(inv.document_number.as_deref(), Some(inv_no.as_str()));
    let t = assert_official("01-rechnung", &bytes(&inv), Some(&inv_no));
    for needle in [
        "GOZ",
        "Faktor",
        "2410",
        "Zwischensumme",
        "Rechnungsbetrag",
        "geb. 15.01.1990",
        "Leistungszeitraum",
        "Steuer-Nr. 66/123/45678",
        "Kammer: Zahnärztekammer Niedersachsen",
        "Tel. 0541 123456",
        "unter Angabe der Rechnungsnummer",
        "4 Nr. 14",
        "Zahlbar innerhalb von 30 Tagen",
    ] {
        assert!(t.contains(&latin1(needle)), "invoice: missing {needle:?}");
    }
    // § 10 Abs. 3 GOZ justification is printed next to the 3.0 factor.
    assert!(t.contains("Kan"), "invoice: factor justification missing");

    // ---- Cancellation (Stornorechnung) ------------------------------------
    let storno = cancel_invoice(
        &pool,
        &s,
        inv.document_id.as_deref().unwrap(),
        "Rechnungsadresse falsch",
    )
    .await
    .expect("cancel");
    let st_no = format!("ST-{year}-000001");
    let t = assert_official("02-stornorechnung", &bytes(&storno), Some(&st_no));
    assert!(
        t.contains(&inv_no),
        "storno must reference the original invoice"
    );

    // ---- Certificate (AU): patient copy and employer copy -------------------
    let cert_layout = r#"{
      "kind": "certificate",
      "documentTitle": "ARBEITSUNFÄHIGKEITSBESCHEINIGUNG",
      "metaLines": [{"label": "Ausgestellt am", "value": "09.11.2026"}],
      "introParagraphs": ["Hiermit wird bescheinigt, dass die unten genannte Person arbeitsunfähig ist."],
      "twoColumn": {"leftTitle": "Patient/in", "leftLines": ["Lena Müller", "Geb.-Datum: 15.01.1990"],
                    "rightTitle": "Anschrift", "rightLines": ["Musterweg 3, 49076 Osnabrück"]},
      "labelValueRows": [
        {"label": "Festgestellt am", "value": "09.11.2026"},
        {"label": "Arbeitsunfähig von", "value": "09.11.2026"},
        {"label": "Voraussichtlich bis einschließlich", "value": "11.11.2026"},
        {"label": "Diagnose (ICD-10)", "value": "K04.0 Pulpitis", "confidential": true},
        {"label": "Befund", "value": "Akute Pulpitis 36", "confidential": true}
      ]
    }"#;
    let cert = |variant: &str| IssueClinicalArgs {
        kind: "certificate".into(),
        source_id: "c1".into(),
        layout_json: cert_layout.into(),
        preview: false,
        copy_variant: Some(variant.into()),
    };
    let patient_copy = issue_clinical_document(&pool, &s, cert("patient"))
        .await
        .expect("AU patient");
    let at_no = patient_copy.document_number.clone().unwrap();
    let t = assert_official("03-au-patient", &bytes(&patient_copy), Some(&at_no));
    assert!(t.contains("K04.0"), "patient copy keeps the diagnosis");
    // Patient identity comes from the database, not from the client layout.
    for needle in ["Lena Müller", "Geb.-Datum: 15.01.1990", "Vers.-Nr.: A123456789"] {
        assert!(t.contains(needle), "AU: missing {needle:?}");
    }

    let employer_copy = issue_clinical_document(&pool, &s, cert("employer"))
        .await
        .expect("AU employer");
    let t = assert_official(
        "04-au-arbeitgeber",
        &bytes(&employer_copy),
        employer_copy.document_number.as_deref(),
    );
    assert!(
        !t.contains("K04.0") && !t.contains("Pulpitis"),
        "employer copy must not show the diagnosis"
    );
    assert!(t.contains("Arbeitgeber"), "employer copy is labelled");
    assert!(t.contains("Lena Müller"), "employer copy names the patient");
    assert!(!t.contains("A123456789"), "employer copy omits the insurance number");

    // ---- Private prescription (AMVV § 2) ----------------------------------
    let rx_layout = r#"{
      "kind": "prescription",
      "documentTitle": "PRIVATREZEPT",
      "metaLines": [{"label": "Ausstellungsdatum", "value": "09.11.2026"}],
      "twoColumn": {"leftTitle": "Patient/in", "leftLines": ["Lena Müller", "Geb.-Datum: 15.01.1990"],
                    "rightTitle": "Anschrift", "rightLines": ["Musterweg 3, 49076 Osnabrück"]},
      "tables": [{"title": "Rp.", "columnLayout": "prescription",
        "headers": ["Arzneimittel / Stärke", "Darreichungsform", "Packung", "Dosierung", "PZN"],
        "rows": [["Ibuprofen 600 mg", "Filmtabletten", "1 × N1 (20 St.)", "1-0-1 nach dem Essen", "—"]]}],
      "labelValueRows": [{"label": "Dauer", "value": "5 Tage"},
                         {"label": "Aut idem", "value": "Austausch zulässig"}],
      "closingParagraphs": ["Privatrezept: gültig 3 Monate ab Ausstellungsdatum."]
    }"#;
    let rx = issue_clinical_document(
        &pool,
        &s,
        IssueClinicalArgs {
            kind: "prescription".into(),
            source_id: "rx1".into(),
            layout_json: rx_layout.into(),
            preview: false,
            copy_variant: None,
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("prescription");
    let t = assert_official(
        "05-privatrezept",
        &bytes(&rx),
        rx.document_number.as_deref(),
    );
    for needle in [
        "Ibuprofen 600 mg",
        "Filmtabletten",
        "1-0-1",
        "Dr. med. dent. Anna Berger",
        "ZANR 987654321",
    ] {
        assert!(t.contains(needle), "prescription: missing {needle:?}");
    }

    // ---- Receipt, reprint, preview ---------------------------------------
    let receipt_layout = r#"{
      "kind": "receipt",
      "documentTitle": "QUITTUNG",
      "metaLines": [{"label": "Zahlungsdatum", "value": "09.11.2026"}, {"label": "Zahlart", "value": "Karte"}],
      "addressLines": ["Lena Müller", "Musterweg 3", "49076 Osnabrück"],
      "tables": [{"columnLayout": "receipt", "headers": ["Datum", "Leistung", "Betrag"],
                  "rows": [["02.11.2026", "Wurzelkanalbehandlung 36", "112,24 EUR"], ["02.11.2026", "Anästhesie", "7,76 EUR"]]}],
      "totals": [{"label": "Rechnungsbetrag", "value": "120,00 EUR"},
                 {"label": "Erhaltener Betrag", "value": "120,00 EUR", "total": true},
                 {"label": "Noch offen", "value": "0,00 EUR"}],
      "closingParagraphs": ["Umsatzsteuerfrei gem. § 4 Nr. 14 Buchst. a UStG.", "Betrag dankend erhalten."]
    }"#;
    let receipt = |preview| IssueClinicalArgs {
        kind: "receipt".into(),
        source_id: "pay1".into(),
        layout_json: receipt_layout.into(),
        preview,
        copy_variant: None,
    };
    let preview = issue_clinical_document(&pool, &s, receipt(true))
        .await
        .expect("preview");
    assert!(preview.document_number.is_none());
    let preview_pdf = bytes(&preview);
    std::fs::write(out_dir().join("06-quittung-vorschau.pdf"), &preview_pdf).unwrap();
    assert!(
        text(&preview_pdf).contains("ENTWURF"),
        "preview carries ENTWURF"
    );

    let q = issue_clinical_document(&pool, &s, receipt(false))
        .await
        .expect("receipt");
    let qu_no = format!("QU-{year}-000001");
    let t = assert_official("07-quittung", &bytes(&q), Some(&qu_no));
    assert!(
        t.contains("Erhaltener Betrag") && t.contains("120,00"),
        "receipt totals"
    );

    let copy = issue_clinical_document(&pool, &s, receipt(false))
        .await
        .expect("reprint");
    assert!(copy.reprint);
    let t = assert_official("08-quittung-kopie", &bytes(&copy), Some(&qu_no));
    assert!(t.contains("KOPIE"), "reprint carries KOPIE");

    // ---- Daily report (Tagesabschluss) -----------------------------------
    let report = ReportPdfInput {
        doc_title: "Tagesabschluss 09.11.2026".into(),
        generated_at: "09.11.2026 18:05".into(),
        report_kind: Some("daily_report".into()),
        summary: vec![
            ReportPdfSummaryRow {
                label: "Einnahmen des Tages".into(),
                value: "120,00 EUR".into(),
            },
            ReportPdfSummaryRow {
                label: "Anzahl Zahlungen".into(),
                value: "1".into(),
            },
        ],
        sections: vec![ReportPdfSection {
            title: "Zahlungen des Tages".into(),
            headers: vec![
                "Uhrzeit".into(),
                "Patient/in".into(),
                "Zahlart".into(),
                "Betrag".into(),
            ],
            rows: vec![vec![
                "10:42".into(),
                "Lena Müller".into(),
                "CARD".into(),
                "120,00 EUR".into(),
            ]],
        }],
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let tb = issue_report(&pool, &s, report, Some("2026-11-09".into()), false)
        .await
        .expect("report");
    let tb_no = format!("TB-{year}-000001");
    assert_eq!(tb.document_number.as_deref(), Some(tb_no.as_str()));
    std::fs::write(out_dir().join("09-tagesabschluss.pdf"), bytes(&tb)).unwrap();

    eprintln!("PDF samples written to {}", out_dir().display());
}

================================================================================
FILE: crates/shared/medoc-core/tests/document_registry_tests.rs
